import React from 'react';
import { NavLink, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Logo } from '../common/Logo';
import {
  Users,
  BookOpen,
  Settings,
  LogOut,
  ExternalLink,
  ShieldCheck,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';

export const AdminSidebar: React.FC<{ onCloseMobile?: () => void }> = ({ onCloseMobile }) => {
  const { user, isSuperAdmin, signOut } = useAuth();

  const navItems = [
    { label: 'Applications', path: '/admin', icon: Users, exact: true },
    { label: 'Courses', path: '/admin/courses', icon: BookOpen },
    { label: 'Settings', path: '/admin/settings', icon: Settings },
  ];

  return (
    <aside className="w-64 bg-slate-950 text-white flex flex-col h-full border-r border-slate-800 shrink-0">
      {/* Brand Header */}
      <div className="p-5 border-b border-slate-800">
        <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Logo variant="light" size="sm" />
        </Link>
        <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold text-red-500">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Admin Panel</span>
          </span>
          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-300">
            {isSuperAdmin ? 'Super Admin' : 'Admin'}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Simple Navigation Links */}
      <nav className="flex-1 p-3 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto">
        {navItems.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;
          return (
            <NavLink
              key={item.path}
              to={item.path}
              end={item.exact}
              onClick={onCloseMobile}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-red-600 text-white shadow-md'
                    : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-900'
                }`
              }
            >
              <Icon className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{item.label}</span>
            </NavLink>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      {/* User Footer & Logout */}
      <div className="p-4 border-t border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/80 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <p className="font-bold text-white truncate text-xs">{user?.full_name || 'Administrator'}</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate">{user?.email}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1 border-t border-slate-900">
          <Link
            to="/"
            target="_blank"
            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-900 transition"
          >
            <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>View Website</span>
          </Link>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => signOut()}
            className="flex items-center justify-center p-2 text-red-400 hover:text-red-300 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-950/40 transition cursor-pointer"
            title="Sign Out"
          >
            <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/components/admin/CourseEditorModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, BookOpen, Clock, Laptop, Image, Save, Eye, EyeOff, Terminal, ListOrdered, Tag, Globe, Copy, Check, Zap } from 'lucide-react';
import { Course, CourseStatus, DurationUnit } from '../../types';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';

interface CourseEditorModalProps {
  course?: Course | null;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const CourseEditorModal: React.FC<CourseEditorModalProps> = ({ course, onClose }) => {
  const { saveCourse, courses } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  const isEditing = Boolean(course?.id);

  // Form states based on requirements:
  // - Course Name
  // - Short Description
  // - Pricing (Original Price & Now Price)
  // - Duration
  // - Learning Mode
  // - Course Image
  // - Hacker Banner Text
  // - Display Order (1, 2, 3...)
  // - Publish / Hide Course
  const [courseName, setCourseName] = useState(course?.title || '');
  const [shortDescription, setShortDescription] = useState(course?.summary || '');
  const [nowPrice, setNowPrice] = useState<number | string>(
    course?.fee_amount !== undefined ? course.fee_amount : 499
  );
  const [originalPrice, setOriginalPrice] = useState<number | string>(
    course?.original_price !== undefined ? course.original_price : 4999
  );
  
  // Program Duration: Value and Unit ('Weeks' | 'Days')
  const initialDurationUnit: DurationUnit = course?.duration_unit
    ? course.duration_unit
    : (course?.duration_text?.toLowerCase().includes('day') ? 'Days' : 'Weeks');

  const initialDurationValue = course?.duration_value !== undefined
    ? course.duration_value
    : (course?.duration_text ? (parseInt(course.duration_text, 10) || course.duration_weeks || 12) : (course?.duration_weeks || 12));

  const [durationValue, setDurationValue] = useState<number | string>(initialDurationValue);
  const [durationUnit, setDurationUnit] = useState<DurationUnit>(initialDurationUnit);

  const [learningMode, setLearningMode] = useState(course?.learning_mode || course?.tagline || 'Live Online');
  const [customSlug, setCustomSlug] = useState(course?.slug || '');
  const [autoOpenModal, setAutoOpenModal] = useState<boolean>(course?.auto_open_modal ?? false);
  const [copiedLink, setCopiedLink] = useState(false);
  const [bannerText, setBannerText] = useState(course?.badge_text || 'Professional Certification Track');
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState<number>(course?.sort_order || courses.length + 1);
  const [courseImage, setCourseImage] = useState(course?.image_url || '');
  const [isPublished, setIsPublished] = useState(course?.status !== 'draft');

  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!courseName.trim()) {
      notifyError('Please enter a course name.');
      return;
    }

    setIsSaving(true);

    const generatedSlug = (customSlug.trim() || courseName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/(^-|-$)/g, ''))
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, '');

    const numDuration = Math.max(1, Number(durationValue) || 1);
    const calculatedWeeks = durationUnit === 'Weeks' ? numDuration : Math.max(1, Math.round(numDuration / 7));
    const calculatedDurationText = `${numDuration} ${durationUnit}`;

    const coursePayload: Partial<Course> = {
      ...(course?.id ? { id: course.id } : {}),
      title: courseName.trim(),
      slug: generatedSlug,
      auto_open_modal: autoOpenModal,
      summary: shortDescription.trim(),
      description: shortDescription.trim(),
      tagline: learningMode.trim(),
      learning_mode: learningMode.trim(),
      fee_amount: Number(nowPrice) || 0,
      original_price: Number(originalPrice) || 0,
      badge_text: bannerText.trim() || 'Professional Certification Track',
      sort_order: Number(sortOrder) || 1,
      duration_value: numDuration,
      duration_unit: durationUnit,
      duration_text: calculatedDurationText,
      duration_weeks: calculatedWeeks,
      hours_per_week: course?.hours_per_week || 8,
      level: course?.level || 'Intermediate',
      currency: 'INR',
      status: (isPublished ? 'published' : 'draft') as CourseStatus,
      featured: course?.featured ?? true,
      image_url: courseImage.trim() || undefined,
      certification_title: course?.certification_title || `EV Certified ${courseName.trim()}`,
      syllabus: course?.syllabus || [],
      outcomes: course?.outcomes || [],
      prerequisites: course?.prerequisites || [],
      tools_covered: course?.tools_covered || [],
    };

    const result = await saveCourse(coursePayload);
    setIsSaving(false);

    if (result.success) {
      success(isEditing ? 'Course updated successfully.' : 'Course created successfully.');
      onClose();
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to save course', result.error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6"
    >
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden animate-fade-in">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="p-6 bg-slate-950 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-red-600/20 text-red-500 flex items-center justify-center">
              <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">
                {isEditing ? 'Edit Course' : 'Add New Course'}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">EV Cyber Academy Private Limited</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Clean, Simple Form */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-5 text-xs">
          {/* 1. Course Name */}
          <div>
            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
              Course Name <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              placeholder="e.g. Certified SOC Analyst & Threat Hunter (L1/L2)"
              value={courseName}
              onChange={(e) => setCourseName(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* 2. Short Description */}
          {/* 2. Course Description */}
          <div>
            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
              Course Description <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={4}
              required
              placeholder="Enter course description, learning outcomes, and practical objectives..."
              value={shortDescription}
              onChange={(e) => setShortDescription(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 resize-y min-h-[110px] leading-relaxed text-xs"
            />
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">
              The full description will be shown completely on the public course card without being cut off.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Custom Campaign URL Route / Slug & Direct Form Settings */}
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-slate-100 space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                <Globe className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />
                Custom URL Route / Slug (Campaign Landing Page)
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Direct Ad Route
              </span>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1 text-[10px]">
                Custom URL Route / Slug <span className="text-red-400">*</span>
              </label>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="relative flex-1">
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-500 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none text-xs">
                    /
                  </span>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    placeholder="e.g. 499 or ethical-hacking-workshop"
                    value={customSlug}
                    onChange={(e) => setCustomSlug(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, ''))}
                    className="w-full pl-7 pr-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 text-white font-mono text-xs font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/30 focus:border-red-500"
                  />
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    const origin = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : 'https://registration01.evcyberacademy.in';
                    const activeSlug = customSlug.trim() || (courseName ? courseName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '') : '499');
                    const fullUrl = `${origin}/${activeSlug}`;
                    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
                      navigator.clipboard.writeText(fullUrl);
                      setCopiedLink(true);
                      setTimeout(() => setCopiedLink(false), 2000);
                    }
                  }}
                  className="px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer border border-slate-700 shrink-0"
                  title="Copy full campaign ad destination URL"
                >
                  {copiedLink ? (
                    <>
                      <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                      <span className="text-emerald-400">Copied!</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                      <span>Copy Link</span>
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Live Preview Badge */}
              <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex-wrap">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Live Preview:</span>
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-red-400 font-mono font-bold truncate max-w-full">
                  {typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : 'https://registration01.evcyberacademy.in'}/
                  {customSlug.trim() || (courseName ? courseName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '') : '499')}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Auto-Open Registration Form Toggle */}
            <div className="pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-800">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="pr-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold text-white text-xs">
                    <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Auto-Open Registration Form on Page Load</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5 leading-snug">
                    Instantly pops up the registration modal when visitors open your custom ad link for maximum conversion.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  role="switch"
                  aria-checked={autoOpenModal}
                  onClick={() => setAutoOpenModal(!autoOpenModal)}
                  className={`relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 shrink-0 cursor-pointer rounded-full border-2 border-transparent transition-colors duration-200 ease-in-out focus:outline-none ${
                    autoOpenModal ? 'bg-red-600' : 'bg-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span
                    className={`pointer-events-none inline-block h-5 w-5 transform rounded-full bg-white shadow-lg ring-0 transition duration-200 ease-in-out ${
                      autoOpenModal ? 'translate-x-5' : 'translate-x-0'
                    }`}
                  />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 3. Course Pricing (Original Price & Now Price) */}
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/90 space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Tag className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600" />
                Course Pricing (₹ INR)
              </span>
              {Number(originalPrice) > Number(nowPrice) && Number(originalPrice) > 0 && (
                <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-300 text-emerald-800 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold">
                  {Math.round(((Number(originalPrice) - Number(nowPrice)) / Number(originalPrice)) * 100)}% DISCOUNT
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                  Original Price (₹ MRP)
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none">
                    ₹
                  </span>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={0}
                    placeholder="e.g. 4999"
                    value={originalPrice}
                    onChange={(e) => setOriginalPrice(e.target.value === '' ? '' : Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full pl-8 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 font-mono font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 bg-white"
                  />
                </div>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">
                  Displayed with strikethrough (Original Price)
                </p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                  Now Price (₹ Current Fee) <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-red-500 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none">
                    ₹
                  </span>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={0}
                    required
                    placeholder="e.g. 499"
                    value={nowPrice}
                    onChange={(e) => setNowPrice(e.target.value === '' ? '' : Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full pl-8 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 font-mono font-black focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 bg-white"
                  />
                </div>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">
                  Active fee students pay (Now Price)
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 4. Duration Selector & 5. Learning Mode Row */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  Program Duration <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 border border-slate-300 text-slate-800 text-[11px] font-mono font-bold">
                  {durationValue || 0} {durationUnit}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="relative flex-1">
                  <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={1}
                    max={durationUnit === 'Weeks' ? 104 : 365}
                    required
                    placeholder={durationUnit === 'Weeks' ? "e.g. 12" : "e.g. 14 or 30"}
                    value={durationValue}
                    onChange={(e) => setDurationValue(e.target.value === '' ? '' : Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full pl-10 pr-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 bg-white"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Duration Unit Selector: Weeks vs Days */}
                <div className="flex rounded-xl bg-slate-100 p-1 border border-slate-300 shrink-0">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDurationUnit('Weeks')}
                    className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                      durationUnit === 'Weeks'
                        ? 'bg-red-600 text-white shadow-xs'
                        : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Weeks
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDurationUnit('Days')}
                    className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                      durationUnit === 'Days'
                        ? 'bg-red-600 text-white shadow-xs'
                        : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Days
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Choose <strong>Weeks</strong> (e.g. 12 Weeks) or <strong>Days</strong> (e.g. 14 Days, 30 Days).
              </p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
                Learning Mode
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Laptop className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  placeholder="e.g. Live Online, Hybrid, Classroom"
                  value={learningMode}
                  onChange={(e) => setLearningMode(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 5. Course Image (Optional) */}
          <div>
            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
              Course Image URL <span className="text-slate-400 font-normal">(Optional)</span>
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Image className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
              <input
                type="url"
                placeholder="https://example.com/course-banner.jpg"
                value={courseImage}
                onChange={(e) => setCourseImage(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 6. Hacker Banner Label / Badge Text (Admin Editable) */}
          <div>
            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
              Card Banner Label / Badge
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Terminal className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                placeholder="e.g. Professional Certification Track"
                value={bannerText}
                onChange={(e) => setBannerText(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 text-xs"
              />
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">
              Displayed on the hacker-themed banner of this course card (e.g. Professional Certification Track, 14-Day Crash Program, etc.).
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* 7. Display Order / Card Position (1, 2, 3...) */}
          <div>
            <label className="block font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
              Display Position on Website (1, 2, 3...)
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <ListOrdered className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
              <select
                value={sortOrder}
                onChange={(e) => setSortOrder(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 text-xs bg-white"
              >
                {Array.from(
                  { length: Math.max(courses.length + (isEditing ? 0 : 1), 6) },
                  (_, i) => i + 1
                ).map((pos) => (
                  <option key={pos} value={pos}>
                    Position #{pos}{' '}
                    {pos === 1
                      ? '(1st Card on Website)'
                      : pos === 2
                      ? '(2nd Card)'
                      : pos === 3
                      ? '(3rd Card)'
                      : ''}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">
              Controls whether this course appears 1st, 2nd, 3rd, etc. on the website.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* 8. Publish / Hide Course Toggle */}
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">Website Publication</p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                {isPublished ? 'This course will be visible to students on the website.' : 'This course is hidden from the public website.'}
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsPublished(!isPublished)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer ${
                isPublished
                  ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200'
                  : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'
              }`}
            >
              {isPublished ? (
                <>
                  <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Published</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Hidden</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Actions */}
          <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-100">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 font-semibold transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isSaving}
              className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 disabled:opacity-50 text-white font-bold transition flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer shadow"
            >
              <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>{isSaving ? 'Saving...' : isEditing ? 'Save Changes' : 'Create Course'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/components/admin/DeleteAllLeadsModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, Trash2, X, Loader2, ShieldAlert } from 'lucide-react';

interface DeleteAllLeadsModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onConfirm: () => Promise<void>;
  totalLeads: number;
  isDeleting: boolean;
}

export const DeleteAllLeadsModal: React.FC<DeleteAllLeadsModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
  totalLeads,
  isDeleting,
}) => {
  const [confirmed, setConfirmed] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleConfirm = async () => {
    if (!confirmed || isDeleting) return;
    await onConfirm();
    setConfirmed(false);
  };

  const handleClose = () => {
    if (isDeleting) return;
    setConfirmed(false);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div
        className="relative w-full max-w-lg bg-slate-900 border border-red-500/30 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl text-white space-y-6 overflow-hidden"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Glow accent */}
        <div className="absolute -top-24 -right-24 w-48 h-48 bg-red-600/20 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Modal Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between relative z-10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 text-red-500 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <ShieldAlert className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-white tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
                <span>Delete All Leads</span>
                <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-red-500/20 border border-red-500/40 text-red-400 font-mono">
                  {totalLeads} record{totalLeads === 1 ? '' : 's'}
                </span>
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                Permanent Database Cleanup Action
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleClose}
            disabled={isDeleting}
            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-800 transition disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Warning Body */}
        <div className="space-y-3 relative z-10">
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-red-950/40 border border-red-800/50 text-xs text-red-200/90 space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-red-400">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>Critical Warning</span>
            </div>
            <p className="leading-relaxed">
              You are about to delete <strong className="text-white font-mono">{totalLeads}</strong> registered student lead(s) along with all counselor notes, follow-up timelines, and intake activity logs.
            </p>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-red-300/70">
              This action cannot be undone. Leads will be permanently removed from both Supabase and the Admin CRM.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Confirmation Checkbox */}
          <label className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700/60 hover:border-slate-600 transition cursor-pointer select-none">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={confirmed}
              onChange={(e) => setConfirmed(e.target.checked)}
              disabled={isDeleting}
              className="mt-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded text-red-600 focus:ring-red-500 bg-slate-900 border-slate-700 cursor-pointer"
            />
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-300 font-medium leading-tight">
              I understand this action is permanent and cannot be undone. Delete all lead records now.
            </span>
          </label>
        </div>

        {/* Footer Buttons */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800 relative z-10">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleClose}
            disabled={isDeleting}
            className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition disabled:opacity-40 cursor-pointer"
          >
            Cancel
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleConfirm}
            disabled={!confirmed || isDeleting || totalLeads === 0}
            className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 disabled:bg-red-950 disabled:text-red-400/40 text-white text-xs font-black tracking-wide transition flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-red-600/20 disabled:shadow-none cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            {isDeleting ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-white" />
                <span>Deleting Leads...</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
                <span>Permanently Delete All Leads</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
File: src/components/admin/LeadDetailModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Phone,
  MessageCircle,
  Mail,
  User,
  Building,
  GraduationCap,
  Calendar,
  Send,
  UserCheck,
  Tag,
  History,
  FileText,
  Trash2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Lead, LeadStatus } from '../../types';
import { LeadStatusBadge } from './LeadStatusBadge';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';

interface LeadDetailModalProps {
  lead: Lead;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const LeadDetailModal: React.FC<LeadDetailModalProps> = ({ lead, onClose }) => {
  const {
    courses,
    updateLeadStatus,
    updateLeadFollowUp,
    assignLead,
    getLeadNotes,
    addLeadNote,
    getLeadHistory,
    deleteLead,
  } = useData();

  const { staffList, isSuperAdmin } = useAuth();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  const [currentStatus, setCurrentStatus] = useState<LeadStatus>(lead.status);
  const [statusNote, setStatusNote] = useState('');
  const [followUpDate, setFollowUpDate] = useState(lead.next_follow_up_date || '');
  const [assignedStaffId, setAssignedStaffId] = useState<string>(lead.assigned_to || '');
  const [newNote, setNewNote] = useState('');
  const [isUpdatingStatus, setIsUpdatingStatus] = useState(false);
  const [isAddingNote, setIsAddingNote] = useState(false);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'notes' | 'history' | 'tracking'>('notes');

  const course = courses.find((c) => c.id === lead.course_id);
  const notes = getLeadNotes(lead.id);
  const history = getLeadHistory(lead.id);

  // Clean phone for WhatsApp & Call
  const cleanPhone = lead.phone.replace(/[^0-9+]/g, '');
  const whatsappNumber = cleanPhone.replace(/^\+/, '');
  const whatsappUrl = `https://wa.me/${whatsappNumber}?text=Hi%20${encodeURIComponent(
    lead.full_name
  )},%20greetings%20from%20EV%20Cyber%20Academy.%20I%20am%20reaching%20out%20regarding%20your%20application%20for%20the%20${encodeURIComponent(
    course?.title || lead.course_title || 'Cybersecurity'
  )}%20cohort.`;

  const handleStatusUpdate = async () => {
    if (currentStatus === lead.status) return;
    setIsUpdatingStatus(true);
    const res = await updateLeadStatus(lead.id, currentStatus, statusNote);
    setIsUpdatingStatus(false);
    if (res.success) {
      success('Lead status updated to ' + currentStatus);
      setStatusNote('');
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to update status', res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleFollowUpChange = async (dateVal: string) => {
    setFollowUpDate(dateVal);
    const res = await updateLeadFollowUp(lead.id, dateVal);
    if (res.success) {
      success('Follow-up date scheduled.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to update follow-up date', res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleAssignmentChange = async (newStaffId: string) => {
    setAssignedStaffId(newStaffId);
    const res = await assignLead(lead.id, newStaffId);
    if (res.success) {
      success('Counselor assigned successfully.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to assign counselor', res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleAddNote = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newNote.trim()) return;
    setIsAddingNote(true);
    const res = await addLeadNote(lead.id, newNote);
    setIsAddingNote(false);
    if (res.success) {
      setNewNote('');
      success('Internal counselor note recorded.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to add note', res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to permanently delete lead ${lead.lead_number}?`)) {
      return;
    }
    const res = await deleteLead(lead.id);
    if (res.success) {
      success('Lead deleted.');
      onClose();
    } else {
      notifyError('Delete failed', res.error);
    }
  };

  const statuses: LeadStatus[] = ['New', 'Contacted', 'Interested', 'Converted', 'Not Interested'];

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl max-w-4xl w-full max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden animate-scale-up">
        {/* Modal Top Header */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950 text-white p-6 flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-800">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1.5">
              <span className="font-mono text-xs font-black text-red-400 bg-red-950/80 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-red-500/30">
                {lead.lead_number}
              </span>
              <LeadStatusBadge status={lead.status} size="md" />
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Registered {new Date(lead.created_at).toLocaleString()}
              </span>
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-white">{lead.full_name}</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-red-400 font-semibold mt-0.5">
              {course?.title || lead.course_title}
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-800 transition"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Quick Action Contact Bar */}
        <div className="bg-slate-100 p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            {/* Direct Phone Call */}
            <a
              href={`tel:${cleanPhone}`}
              className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white hover:bg-slate-800 text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
            >
              <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
              <span>Call ({lead.phone})</span>
            </a>

            {/* Direct WhatsApp */}
            <a
              href={whatsappUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 text-white hover:bg-emerald-700 text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
            >
              <MessageCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>WhatsApp Counselor Chat</span>
            </a>

            {/* Direct Email */}
            <a
              href={`mailto:${lead.email}?subject=EV%20Cyber%20Academy%20Admissions%20-%20Application%20Follow-up`}
              className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5"
            >
              <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
              <span>Send Email</span>
            </a>
          </div>

          {/* Super Admin Delete */}
          {isSuperAdmin && (
            <button
              onClick={handleDelete}
              className="text-xs text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 transition"
              title="Delete lead record"
            >
              <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Delete Lead</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Modal Body */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
          {/* Left Column: Lead Info & Pipeline Controls */}
          <div className="md:col-span-5 space-y-5">
            {/* Candidate Details Card */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
                Candidate Profile
              </h3>

              <div className="space-y-2 text-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
                  <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  <span className="font-semibold">{lead.full_name}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
                  <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  <span>{lead.email}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
                  <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  <span>{lead.phone}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
                  <Building className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  <span>{lead.college_company}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
                  <GraduationCap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{lead.experience_level}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Pipeline Stage Management */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
                Pipeline Stage Control
              </h3>

              <div className="space-y-2">
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 block">Current Status:</label>
                <select
                  value={currentStatus}
                  onChange={(e) => setCurrentStatus(e.target.value as LeadStatus)}
                  className="w-full text-xs font-bold p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
                >
                  {statuses.map((st) => (
                    <option key={st} value={st}>
                      {st}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>

                {currentStatus !== lead.status && (
                  <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      placeholder="Optional status transition note..."
                      value={statusNote}
                      onChange={(e) => setStatusNote(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full text-xs p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleStatusUpdate}
                      disabled={isUpdatingStatus}
                      className="w-full py-2 px-3 rounded-lg bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-sm transition"
                    >
                      {isUpdatingStatus ? 'Saving...' : 'Confirm Status Change'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Scheduled Follow-Up Date */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-600" />
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
                  Scheduled Follow-Up Date
                </h3>
              </div>
              <input
                type="date"
                value={followUpDate}
                onChange={(e) => handleFollowUpChange(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full text-xs font-semibold p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Staff Counselor Assignment */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <UserCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600" />
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
                  Assigned Counselor
                </h3>
              </div>

              <select
                value={assignedStaffId}
                onChange={(e) => handleAssignmentChange(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full text-xs font-medium p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
              >
                <option value="">-- Unassigned --</option>
                {staffList.map((st) => (
                  <option key={st.id} value={st.id}>
                    {st.full_name} ({st.role === 'super_admin' ? 'Admin' : 'Counselor'})
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right Column: Tabbed Notes, History, and Tracking */}
          <div className="md:col-span-7 flex flex-col space-y-4">
            {/* Tabs */}
            <div className="flex items-center border-b border-slate-200 gap-4 text-xs font-bold">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('notes')}
                className={`pb-2.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
                  activeTab === 'notes'
                    ? 'border-b-2 border-red-600 text-red-600'
                    : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Counselor Notes ({notes.length})</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('history')}
                className={`pb-2.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
                  activeTab === 'history'
                    ? 'border-b-2 border-red-600 text-red-600'
                    : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                <History className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Status &amp; Audit Trail ({history.length})</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('tracking')}
                className={`pb-2.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition ${
                  activeTab === 'tracking'
                    ? 'border-b-2 border-red-600 text-red-600'
                    : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                <Tag className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Campaign &amp; UTM</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Tab: Internal Notes */}
            {activeTab === 'notes' && (
              <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col space-y-4">
                {/* Add Note Box */}
                <form onSubmit={handleAddNote} className="space-y-2">
                  <textarea
                    rows={3}
                    placeholder="Record notes on candidate call, syllabus shared, lab readiness..."
                    value={newNote}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewNote(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-600/20"
                  />
                  <div className="flex justify-end">
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={isAddingNote || !newNote.trim()}
                      className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 hover:bg-black text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 disabled:opacity-40"
                    >
                      <Send className="w-3 h-3" />
                      <span>{isAddingNote ? 'Saving...' : 'Add Note'}</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>

                {/* Notes List */}
                <div className="space-y-2.5 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                  {notes.length > 0 ? (
                    notes.map((n) => (
                      <div key={n.id} className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          <span className="text-red-600 font-bold">{n.author_name}</span>
                          <span className="text-slate-400 font-normal">
                            {new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleString()}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-slate-800 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap">{n.note}</p>
                      </div>
                    ))
                  ) : (
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic text-center py-6">
                      No counselor notes added yet.
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Tab: History */}
            {activeTab === 'history' && (
              <div className="space-y-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                {history.length > 0 ? (
                  history.map((h) => (
                    <div key={h.id} className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-xs">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        <span className="font-bold">
                          Status &rarr; <span className="text-red-600">{h.new_status}</span>
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-slate-400">
                          {new Date(h.created_at).toLocaleString()}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      {h.changed_by_name && (
                        <p className="text-slate-500 text-[11px]">Changed by: {h.changed_by_name}</p>
                      )}
                      {h.note && <p className="text-slate-700 mt-1 italic">&ldquo;{h.note}&rdquo;</p>}
                    </div>
                  ))
                ) : (
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic text-center py-6">
                    No status history recorded.
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Tab: UTM / Tracking */}
            {activeTab === 'tracking' && (
              <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3 text-xs">
                <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900">Campaign Attribution Data</h4>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200">
                    <span className="text-slate-400 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">UTM Source</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{lead.utm_source || 'direct'}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200">
                    <span className="text-slate-400 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">UTM Medium</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{lead.utm_medium || 'organic'}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200">
                    <span className="text-slate-400 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">UTM Campaign</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{lead.utm_campaign || 'none'}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200">
                    <span className="text-slate-400 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">Referral Source</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{lead.referral_source || 'website'}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/components/admin/LeadStatusBadge.tsx
