import React from 'react';
import { LeadStatus } from '../../types';

interface LeadStatusBadgeProps {
  status: LeadStatus;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
}

export const LeadStatusBadge: React.FC<LeadStatusBadgeProps> = ({ status, size = 'sm' }) => {
  const styles: Record<LeadStatus, string> = {
    New: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200',
    Contacted: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200',
    Interested: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200',
    Converted: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 font-bold',
    'Not Interested': 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border-slate-200',
  };

  const dots: Record<LeadStatus, string> = {
    New: 'bg-blue-500',
    Contacted: 'bg-amber-500',
    Interested: 'bg-purple-500',
    Converted: 'bg-emerald-500',
    'Not Interested': 'bg-slate-400',
  };

  const sizeClasses = size === 'sm' ? 'px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px]' : 'px-3 py-1 text-xs';

  return (
    <span
      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md font-semibold border ${
        styles[status] || 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-200'
      } ${sizeClasses}`}
    >
      <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${dots[status] || 'bg-slate-400'}`} />
      <span>{status}</span>
    </span>
  );
};


================================================================================
File: src/components/admin/LeadTable.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Phone,
  MessageCircle,
  Eye,
  Search,
  Download,
  Calendar,
  Building,
  Clock,
  Trash2,
  Loader2,
  AlertCircle,
  RefreshCw,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Lead } from '../../types';
import { LeadStatusBadge } from './LeadStatusBadge';
import { LeadDetailModal } from './LeadDetailModal';
import { DeleteAllLeadsModal } from './DeleteAllLeadsModal';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';

interface LeadTableProps {
  initialCourseFilter?: string;
}

export const LeadTable: React.FC<LeadTableProps> = ({ initialCourseFilter }) => {
  const {
    leads,
    courses,
    isLoadingLeads,
    leadsFetchError,
    fetchLeads,
    exportLeadsCsv,
    deleteAllLeads,
  } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [courseFilter, setCourseFilter] = useState<string>(initialCourseFilter || 'all');
  const [selectedLead, setSelectedLead] = useState<Lead | null>(null);
  const [showDeleteModal, setShowDeleteModal] = useState(false);
  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

  const handleConfirmDeleteAll = async () => {
    if (leads.length === 0) return;
    setIsDeleting(true);
    const res = await deleteAllLeads();
    setIsDeleting(false);
    setShowDeleteModal(false);

    if (res.success) {
      if (selectedLead) setSelectedLead(null);
      success(`All leads deleted successfully (${res.count || 0} removed).`);
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to delete leads', res.error || 'Operation failed');
    }
  };

  // Filter leads
  const filteredLeads = leads.filter((lead) => {
    // Search match
    if (searchQuery.trim()) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      const matchName = lead.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchEmail = lead.email.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchPhone = lead.phone.includes(q);
      const matchNumber = lead.lead_number.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchCompany = lead.college_company.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      if (!matchName && !matchEmail && !matchPhone && !matchNumber && !matchCompany) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    // Status filter
    if (statusFilter !== 'all' && lead.status !== statusFilter) {
      return false;
    }

    // Course filter
    if (courseFilter !== 'all' && lead.course_id !== courseFilter) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Filters & Export Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        {/* Search */}
        <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-md">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search leads by name, email, phone, reference, organization..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full pl-9 pr-4 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-600/10 focus:border-red-600"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Dropdowns & CSV */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
          {/* Course filter */}
          <select
            value={courseFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setCourseFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
          >
            <option value="all">All Cohorts &amp; Programs</option>
            {courses.map((c) => (
              <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                {c.title}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {/* Status filter */}
          <select
            value={statusFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white"
          >
            <option value="all">All Pipeline Stages</option>
            <option value="New">New Applications</option>
            <option value="Contacted">Contacted</option>
            <option value="Interested">Interested</option>
            <option value="Converted">Converted</option>
            <option value="Not Interested">Not Interested</option>
          </select>

          {/* CSV Export */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => exportLeadsCsv({ courseId: courseFilter, status: statusFilter })}
            className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-black text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-400" />
            <span>Export CSV</span>
          </button>

          {/* Delete All Leads */}
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={leads.length === 0 || isDeleting}
            onClick={() => setShowDeleteModal(true)}
            className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-red-600 border border-red-200 text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
            title="Permanently delete all registered leads"
          >
            <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-600" />
            <span>Delete All</span>
          </button>
          {/* Refresh Leads from DB */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => fetchLeads()}
            disabled={isLoadingLeads}
            className="p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 transition cursor-pointer"
            title="Refresh leads from database"
          >
            <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isLoadingLeads ? 'animate-spin text-red-600' : ''}`} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Database Error Banner */}
      {leadsFetchError && (
        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 shrink-0" />
            <span>Database Connection Error: {leadsFetchError}</span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => fetchLeads()}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer shadow-xs"
          >
            <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Retry</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Leads Table */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
            <thead>
              <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Lead Ref</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Candidate Details</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Selected Program</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Experience</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Pipeline Status</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Counselor</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Follow-Up Date</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Registered</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Instant Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 font-medium text-slate-700">
              {filteredLeads.length > 0 ? (
                filteredLeads.map((lead) => {
                  const crs = courses.find((c) => c.id === lead.course_id);
                  const cleanPhone = lead.phone.replace(/[^0-9+]/g, '');
                  const whatsappUrl = `https://wa.me/${cleanPhone.replace(
                    /^\+/,
                    ''
                  )}?text=Hi%20${encodeURIComponent(
                    lead.full_name
                  )},%20greetings%20from%20EV%20Cyber%20Academy.`;

                  return (
                    <tr key={lead.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                      {/* Ref */}
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">
                        {lead.lead_number}
                      </td>

                      {/* Candidate info */}
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 min-w-[200px]">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setSelectedLead(lead)}
                          className="font-bold text-slate-900 hover:text-red-600 text-left block"
                        >
                          {lead.full_name}
                        </button>
                        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                          <span>{lead.email}</span>
                          <span>&bull;</span>
                          <span>{lead.phone}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                          <Building className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                          <span className="truncate">{lead.college_company}</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      {/* Course */}
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 max-w-[220px]">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 line-clamp-1">
                          {crs?.title || lead.course_title}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block mt-0.5">
                          Fee: ₹{crs?.fee_amount.toLocaleString('en-IN') || '49,999'}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* Experience */}
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <span className="inline-block px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 text-[11px]">
                          {lead.experience_level}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* Status */}
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <LeadStatusBadge status={lead.status} />
                      </td>

                      {/* Counselor */}
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 whitespace-nowrap text-slate-600 text-[11px]">
                        {lead.assigned_staff_name || 'Unassigned'}
                      </td>

                      {/* Follow-up date */}
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 whitespace-nowrap text-[11px]">
                        {lead.next_follow_up_date ? (
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-700 font-medium">
                            <Clock className="w-3 h-3 text-red-500" />
                            <span>{new Date(lead.next_follow_up_date).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-slate-400">Not set</span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      {/* Registration Date */}
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 whitespace-nowrap text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        {new Date(lead.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}
                      </td>

                      {/* Actions */}
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                          {/* Call */}
                          <a
                            href={`tel:${cleanPhone}`}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:text-slate-900 transition"
                            title="Direct Call"
                          >
                            <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                          </a>

                          {/* WhatsApp */}
                          <a
                            href={whatsappUrl}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 transition"
                            title="Chat on WhatsApp"
                          >
                            <MessageCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                          </a>

                          {/* View details */}
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setSelectedLead(lead)}
                            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-bold text-[11px] transition flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                          >
                            <Eye className="w-3 h-3" />
                            <span>Details</span>
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })
              ) : (
                isLoadingLeads ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={9} className="py-12 text-center text-slate-500 font-normal">
                      <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin mx-auto text-red-600 mb-2" />
                      <span>Retrieving live leads from database...</span>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={9} className="py-12 text-center text-slate-400 font-normal">
                      No leads found matching your criteria.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        {/* Footer info */}
        <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span>Showing {filteredLeads.length} of {leads.length} recorded leads</span>
          <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Authenticated staff access protected by Supabase RLS</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Lead Detail Modal */}
      {selectedLead && (
        <LeadDetailModal
          lead={selectedLead}
          onClose={() => setSelectedLead(null)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Delete All Leads Modal */}
      <DeleteAllLeadsModal
        isOpen={showDeleteModal}
        onClose={() => setShowDeleteModal(false)}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmDeleteAll}
        totalLeads={leads.length}
        isDeleting={isDeleting}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/components/admin/VideoEditorModal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Video,
  Play,
  ShieldCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  Eye,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { VideoItem, VideoPlacement } from '../../types';
import { extractYouTubeId, buildYouTubeEmbedUrl } from '../../lib/youtube';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';
import { YouTubeEmbed } from '../common/YouTubeEmbed';

interface VideoEditorModalProps {
  video?: VideoItem | null;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const VideoEditorModal: React.FC<VideoEditorModalProps> = ({ video, onClose }) => {
  const { courses, saveVideo } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: video?.title || '',
    description: video?.description || '',
    youtube_url: video?.youtube_url || '',
    placement: (video?.placement || 'hero') as VideoPlacement,
    pinned_homepage: video?.pinned_homepage ?? false,
    pinned_course_id: video?.pinned_course_id || '',
    is_active: video?.is_active ?? true,
    autoplay: video?.autoplay ?? true,
    muted_autoplay: video?.muted_autoplay ?? true,
    show_controls: video?.show_controls ?? true,
    sort_order: video?.sort_order ?? 0,
  });

  const [previewMode, setPreviewMode] = useState(false);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  const parsedId = extractYouTubeId(formData.youtube_url);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!formData.title.trim()) {
      notifyError('Video title is required.');
      return;
    }

    if (!parsedId) {
      notifyError('Please enter a valid YouTube URL or 11-character Video ID.');
      return;
    }

    setIsSaving(true);
    const res = await saveVideo({
      id: video?.id,
      title: formData.title,
      description: formData.description,
      youtube_url: formData.youtube_url,
      youtube_video_id: parsedId,
      placement: formData.placement,
      pinned_homepage: formData.pinned_homepage,
      pinned_course_id: formData.pinned_course_id || null,
      is_active: formData.is_active,
      autoplay: formData.autoplay,
      muted_autoplay: formData.muted_autoplay,
      show_controls: formData.show_controls,
      sort_order: Number(formData.sort_order),
    });
    setIsSaving(false);

    if (res.success) {
      success('Video saved successfully.');
      onClose();
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to save video', res.error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl max-w-2xl w-full max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden animate-scale-up">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950 text-white p-6 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-red-600/20 text-red-500 border border-red-500/30 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Video className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-white">
                {video ? 'Edit YouTube Stream' : 'Deploy New YouTube Stream'}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Admin-Controlled Video Management</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-800 transition"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Body */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
          {/* Live Preview / Mode Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-200">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
              Live Embed Preview
            </span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setPreviewMode(!previewMode)}
              className="text-xs font-bold text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{previewMode ? 'Return to Config' : 'Preview Player'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {previewMode ? (
            <div className="p-2 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
              {parsedId ? (
                <YouTubeEmbed
                  urlOrId={parsedId}
                  title={formData.title || 'Preview Player'}
                  description={formData.description}
                  autoplay={formData.autoplay}
                  muted={formData.muted_autoplay}
                  showControls={formData.show_controls}
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="py-12 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
                  Provide a valid YouTube link or ID to see live preview.
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form id="video-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
              {/* YouTube URL or ID */}
              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  YouTube Video URL or 11-char ID <span className="text-red-600">*</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  placeholder="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=inWWhr5tnEA or inWWhr5tnEA"
                  value={formData.youtube_url}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, youtube_url: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-mono text-xs focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-600/20 focus:border-red-600"
                />
                {parsedId ? (
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-emerald-600 font-medium mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>Parsed Video ID: <strong>{parsedId}</strong> (Privacy-Enhanced Nocookie Domain Ready)</span>
                  </p>
                ) : formData.youtube_url ? (
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-red-600 font-medium mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>Could not extract 11-character YouTube video ID.</span>
                  </p>
                ) : null}
              </div>

              {/* Title & Description */}
              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Video Headline / Title <span className="text-red-600">*</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  placeholder="e.g. Inside the EV Cyber War Room: Live Range Walkthrough"
                  value={formData.title}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, title: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-600/20 focus:border-red-600"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Description / Context
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  placeholder="Briefly describe what candidates will see in this video..."
                  value={formData.description}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, description: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-600/20 focus:border-red-600"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Placement & Pinned Course */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Display Placement
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={formData.placement}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setFormData({ ...formData, placement: e.target.value as VideoPlacement })
                    }
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white font-medium"
                  >
                    <option value="hero">Hero (Front and Center)</option>
                    <option value="after_hero">After Hero / Range Tour</option>
                    <option value="course_details">Course Details Page</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Pin to Specific Program (Optional)
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={formData.pinned_course_id}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, pinned_course_id: e.target.value })}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white font-medium"
                  >
                    <option value="">-- None (Global) --</option>
                    {courses.map((c) => (
                      <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                        {c.title}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Switches & Controls */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3">
                <span className="font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 block">
                  Autoplay &amp; Playback Policies
                </span>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                  {/* Pin to homepage */}
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={formData.pinned_homepage}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setFormData({ ...formData, pinned_homepage: e.target.checked })
                      }
                      className="rounded text-red-600 focus:ring-red-500"
                    />
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">Pin to Homepage Hero</span>
                  </label>

                  {/* Active / Publish */}
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={formData.is_active}
                      onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, is_active: e.target.checked })}
                      className="rounded text-red-600 focus:ring-red-500"
                    />
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">Active / Published</span>
                  </label>

                  {/* Autoplay */}
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={formData.autoplay}
                      onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, autoplay: e.target.checked })}
                      className="rounded text-red-600 focus:ring-red-500"
                    />
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">Enable Autoplay</span>
                  </label>

                  {/* Muted Autoplay */}
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={formData.muted_autoplay}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setFormData({ ...formData, muted_autoplay: e.target.checked })
                      }
                      className="rounded text-red-600 focus:ring-red-500"
                    />
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">Muted Autoplay (Browser Policy)</span>
                  </label>

                  {/* Show controls */}
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={formData.show_controls}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setFormData({ ...formData, show_controls: e.target.checked })
                      }
                      className="rounded text-red-600 focus:ring-red-500"
                    />
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">Always Show Player Controls</span>
                  </label>
                </div>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200 transition"
          >
            Cancel
          </button>

          <button
            type="submit"
            form="video-form"
            disabled={isSaving}
            className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md transition disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {isSaving ? 'Deploying...' : video ? 'Update Stream' : 'Deploy YouTube Stream'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Logo } from './Logo';
import { Menu, X, ArrowRight, Lock, PhoneCall } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';

export const Header: React.FC = () => {
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const { isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const { siteSettings } = useData();

  const scrollToSection = (id: string) => {
    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
    const element = document.getElementById(id);
    if (element) {
      element.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-slate-950/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-slate-800/80 shadow-lg">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between h-20">
          {/* Official Logo */}
          <Link
            to="/"
            onClick={() => window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })}
            className="flex items-center focus:outline-none"
          >
            <Logo variant="light" size="md" />
          </Link>

          {/* Desktop Navigation */}
          <nav className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-8">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => scrollToSection('courses')}
              className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:text-red-400 transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              Courses &amp; Programs
            </button>
            {siteSettings.show_intro_video !== false && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => scrollToSection('overview')}
                className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:text-red-400 transition cursor-pointer"
              >
                Overview Video
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => scrollToSection('contact')}
              className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:text-red-400 transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              Contact Us
            </button>
          </nav>

          {/* Action CTAs */}
          <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => scrollToSection('contact')}
              className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 hover:text-white border border-slate-700/80 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 hover:bg-slate-800 transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              Contact for Courses
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => scrollToSection('courses')}
              className="px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 to-rose-600 hover:from-red-500 hover:to-rose-500 active:from-red-700 rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-red-950/50 transition flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <span>Apply Now</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>

            {isAuthenticated && (
              <Link
                to="/admin"
                className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-400 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-900 transition"
                title="Go to Admin Panel"
              >
                <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Mobile menu button */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 md:hidden">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => scrollToSection('courses')}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-red-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm"
            >
              Apply
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}
              className="p-2 text-slate-300 hover:text-white focus:outline-none"
              aria-label="Toggle navigation menu"
            >
              {mobileMenuOpen ? <X className="w-6 h-6" /> : <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Mobile Menu Drawer */}
      {mobileMenuOpen && (
        <div className="md:hidden border-t border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 px-4 pt-3 pb-6 space-y-3 shadow-2xl">
          <nav className="flex flex-col space-y-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => scrollToSection('courses')}
              className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-900 hover:text-red-400 rounded-lg transition"
            >
              Courses &amp; Programs
            </button>
            {siteSettings.show_intro_video !== false && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => scrollToSection('overview')}
                className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-900 hover:text-red-400 rounded-lg transition"
              >
                Overview Video
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => scrollToSection('contact')}
              className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-900 hover:text-red-400 rounded-lg transition"
            >
              Contact Us
            </button>
          </nav>

          <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-800 space-y-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => scrollToSection('courses')}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 to-rose-600 hover:from-red-500 rounded-xl shadow-lg"
            >
              <span>Apply for Course</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => scrollToSection('contact')}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800"
            >
              <PhoneCall className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500" />
              <span>Contact for Courses</span>
            </button>

            {isAuthenticated && (
              <Link
                to="/admin"
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-400 hover:text-red-400"
              >
                <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Admin Panel</span>
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/components/common/Logo.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ShieldAlert } from 'lucide-react';

interface LogoProps {
  className?: string;
  variant?: 'light' | 'dark' | 'auto';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  showSubtitle?: boolean;
}

export const Logo: React.FC<LogoProps> = ({
  className = '',
  variant = 'auto',
  size = 'md',
  showSubtitle = true,
}) => {
  const [imageError, setImageError] = useState(false);

  // Sizing definitions
  const heights = {
    sm: 'h-8',
    md: 'h-10',
    lg: 'h-14',
  };

  const textSizes = {
    sm: 'text-base',
    md: 'text-xl',
    lg: 'text-2xl',
  };

  const isLight = variant === 'light';

  return (
    <div className={`flex items-center gap-3 select-none ${className}`}>
      {!imageError ? (
        <img
          src="/off-logo.jpeg"
          alt="EV Cyber Academy Official Logo"
          className={`${heights[size]} w-auto object-contain rounded-md shadow-sm border border-slate-200/50`}
          onError={() => setImageError(true)}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-red-600 text-white font-black text-xl shadow-md">
          <ShieldAlert className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-col leading-tight">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span
            className={`font-black tracking-tight ${textSizes[size]} ${
              isLight ? 'text-white' : 'text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            EV
          </span>
          <span
            className={`font-black tracking-tight ${textSizes[size]} text-red-600`}
          >
            CYBER
          </span>
          <span
            className={`font-bold tracking-tight ${textSizes[size]} ${
              isLight ? 'text-slate-200' : 'text-slate-800'
            }`}
          >
            ACADEMY
          </span>
        </div>
        {showSubtitle && (
          <span
            className={`text-[10px] tracking-widest font-black uppercase ${
              isLight ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'
            }`}
          >
            Private Limited
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
File: src/components/common/StickyCtaBar.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ShieldCheck, Calendar, Users, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { Course } from '../../types';
import { trackEvent } from '../../lib/analytics';

interface StickyCtaBarProps {
  course?: Course;
  customTitle?: string;
  onApplyClick?: () => void;
}

export const StickyCtaBar: React.FC<StickyCtaBarProps> = ({
  course,
  customTitle,
  onApplyClick,
}) => {
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => {
      // Show when scrolled down more than 350px
      if (window.scrollY > 350) {
        setIsVisible(true);
      } else {
        setIsVisible(false);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
  }, []);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  const targetUrl = course ? `/register?course=${course.id}` : '/register';
  const displayTitle = customTitle || course?.title || 'EV Cyber Defense Cohort';
  const hasRemainingSeats = course?.seats_remaining != null && course.seats_remaining > 0;

  const handleClick = () => {
    trackEvent('cta_click', course?.id, { source: 'sticky_bar' });
    if (onApplyClick) onApplyClick();
  };

  return (
    <aside
      aria-label="Registration bar"
      className="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-40 bg-slate-950/95 backdrop-blur-lg border-t border-red-500/30 text-white py-3 px-4 shadow-2xl transition-transform duration-300 transform translate-y-0 animate-fade-in"
    >
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        {/* Left Info */}
        <div className="min-w-0 flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-red-600/20 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 shrink-0">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <h4 className="text-sm font-bold truncate text-white">{displayTitle}</h4>
              {hasRemainingSeats ? (
                <span className="hidden md:inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-400 bg-amber-950/60 border border-amber-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                  <Users className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>{course?.seats_remaining} seats remaining</span>
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span className="hidden md:inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Admissions Active</span>
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              {course?.batch_start_date && (
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-500" />
                  <span>Next Batch: {new Date(course.batch_start_date).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}</span>
                </span>
              )}
              {(course?.duration_text || course?.duration_weeks) && (
                <span className="hidden sm:inline">&bull; {course.duration_text || `${course.duration_weeks} Weeks`} Intensive</span>
              )}
              {course?.fee_amount ? (
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-200">
                  &bull; ₹{course.fee_amount.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                </span>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right CTA */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
          <Link
            to={targetUrl}
            onClick={handleClick}
            className="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-lg hover:shadow-brand transition-all flex items-center gap-2 group cursor-pointer"
          >
            <span>Apply Now</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" />
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};


================================================================================
File: src/components/common/YouTubeEmbed.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Volume2, VolumeX, Play, ShieldCheck, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';
import { buildYouTubeEmbedUrl, extractYouTubeId } from '../../lib/youtube';
import { trackEvent } from '../../lib/analytics';

interface YouTubeEmbedProps {
  urlOrId: string;
  title: string;
  autoplay?: boolean;
  muted?: boolean;
  showControls?: boolean;
  privacyEnhanced?: boolean;
  courseId?: string;
  className?: string;
  description?: string;
}

export const YouTubeEmbed: React.FC<YouTubeEmbedProps> = ({
  urlOrId,
  title,
  autoplay = true,
  muted = true,
  showControls = true,
  privacyEnhanced = true,
  courseId,
  className = '',
  description,
}) => {
  const [isMuted, setIsMuted] = useState(muted);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(autoplay);
  const [hasInteracted, setHasInteracted] = useState(false);

  const videoId = extractYouTubeId(urlOrId);

  if (!videoId) {
    return (
      <div className="w-full aspect-video bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-400 p-6 text-center">
        <p className="font-semibold text-slate-300">Video currently unavailable</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Please check the YouTube URL or video ID in Admin.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const embedUrl = buildYouTubeEmbedUrl(videoId, {
    autoplay: isPlaying,
    muted: isMuted,
    controls: showControls,
    privacyEnhanced,
  });

  const handleStartPlay = () => {
    setIsPlaying(true);
    setIsMuted(false);
    setHasInteracted(true);
    trackEvent('video_play', courseId, {
      title,
      videoId,
      action: 'play_with_sound',
    });
  };

  const toggleMute = () => {
    setIsMuted((prev) => !prev);
    if (!hasInteracted) {
      setHasInteracted(true);
      trackEvent('video_play', courseId, {
        title,
        videoId,
        action: isMuted ? 'unmute' : 'mute',
      });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={`relative group rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 shadow-2xl ${className}`}>
      {/* 16:9 Aspect Ratio Frame */}
      <div className="relative w-full aspect-video">
        {!isPlaying ? (
          <div
            onClick={handleStartPlay}
            className="w-full h-full relative cursor-pointer group/thumb overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950"
            title="Click to play video"
          >
            {/* Lightweight Thumbnail Preview */}
            <img
              src={`https://i.ytimg.com/vi/${videoId}/hqdefault.jpg`}
              alt={title}
              loading="lazy"
              className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover/thumb:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300 opacity-90 group-hover/thumb:opacity-100"
            />
            {/* Ambient Dark Gradient Overlay */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-slate-950/80 via-transparent to-black/30 pointer-events-none" />

            {/* Glowing Cyber Play Button */}
            <div className="relative z-10 w-16 h-16 sm:w-20 sm:h-20 rounded-full bg-red-600/90 hover:bg-red-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-2xl shadow-red-600/50 group-hover/thumb:scale-110 transition-all duration-300 border-2 border-white/20">
              <Play className="w-8 h-8 sm:w-10 sm:h-10 fill-white translate-x-0.5" />
            </div>

            <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-4 right-4 z-10 flex items-center justify-between text-white text-xs pointer-events-none">
              <span className="font-bold truncate drop-shadow-md">{title}</span>
              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs text-[10px] font-mono border border-white/20 shrink-0">
                Click to Play
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <iframe
            src={embedUrl}
            title={title}
            allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share"
            allowFullScreen
            className="w-full h-full border-0 absolute inset-0"
          />
        )}

        {/* Video overlay controls & unmute helper banner if muted autoplay is active */}
        {isMuted && isPlaying && (
          <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 z-10 flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={toggleMute}
              type="button"
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-red-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold backdrop-blur-md border border-slate-700/80 transition shadow-lg cursor-pointer"
              title="Click to enable sound"
            >
              <VolumeX className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400 group-hover:text-white" />
              <span>Click to Unmute Sound</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Unmute / Audio indicator when unmuted */}
        {!isMuted && isPlaying && (
          <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 z-10">
            <button
              onClick={toggleMute}
              type="button"
              className="p-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-900/80 text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition"
              title="Mute audio"
            >
              <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Video caption / badge footer */}
      <div className="p-4 bg-slate-900/95 border-t border-slate-800/80 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold bg-red-600/20 text-red-400 border border-red-500/30">
              OFFICIAL STREAM
            </span>
            <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-white truncate">{title}</h4>
          </div>
          {description && (
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1 line-clamp-1">{description}</p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>Privacy-Enhanced</span>
          </div>
          <a
            href={`https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=${videoId}`}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-white flex items-center gap-1 transition"
          >
            <span>YouTube</span>
            <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import {
  Clock,
  Laptop,
  ArrowRight,
  Terminal,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Course } from '../../types';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';

interface CourseCardProps {
  course: Course;
  featured?: boolean;
  onApply?: (course: Course) => void;
}

export const CourseCard: React.FC<CourseCardProps> = ({ course, onApply }) => {
  const { siteSettings } = useData();

  // Determine duration text
  const durationText =
    course.duration_text ||
    (course.duration_weeks ? `${course.duration_weeks} Weeks` : 'Self-Paced');

  // Determine learning mode
  const learningMode =
    course.learning_mode || course.tagline || 'Live Online & Hands-on Labs';

  // Determine the banner label text (Admin-Controlled & Editable)
  const bannerLabel =
    course.badge_text ||
    siteSettings.default_card_banner_text ||
    'Professional Certification Track';

  // Pricing: Original Price & Now Price
  const nowPrice = course.fee_amount ?? 0;
  const originalPrice = course.original_price ?? 0;
  const hasDiscount = originalPrice > nowPrice && originalPrice > 0;
  const discountPercent = hasDiscount
    ? Math.round(((originalPrice - nowPrice) / originalPrice) * 100)
    : 0;

  const handleApplyClick = (e?: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (e) e.stopPropagation();
    if (onApply) {
      onApply(course);
    } else {
      const el = document.getElementById('courses');
      if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      onClick={handleApplyClick}
      className="group flex flex-col bg-[#090d16] rounded-3xl border border-slate-800/90 shadow-xl hover:shadow-2xl hover:shadow-red-600/15 hover:border-red-500/50 hover:-translate-y-2 transition-all duration-300 overflow-hidden cursor-pointer relative"
    >
      {/* Top Accent Gradient Bar */}
      <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 via-rose-500 to-amber-500" />

      {/* =====================================================================
          UNIFIED PROFESSIONAL HACKER-THEMED BANNER (SAME FOR ALL COURSES)
      ====================================================================== */}
      <div className="h-44 sm:h-48 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#040711] via-[#080d1a] to-[#140810] relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-between p-5 sm:p-6 border-b border-slate-800/80 select-none">
        {/* Subtle Cyber Grid Matrix Background */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,#ef444415_1px,transparent_1px),linear-gradient(to_bottom,#ef444415_1px,transparent_1px)] bg-[size:1.25rem_1.25rem] pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Ambient Red Glow in Corner */}
        <div className="absolute -top-10 -right-10 w-36 h-36 bg-red-600/20 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Top Terminal Bar */}
        <div className="relative z-10 flex items-center justify-between w-full">
          {/* Terminal Controls & Shell Prompt */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-red-500/90" />
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-amber-500/90" />
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/90" />
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 tracking-wider">
              root@ev-cyber:~#
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Live Batch Indicator */}
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-slate-950/90 backdrop-blur-md border border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold tracking-wider">
            <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
              <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-emerald-400 opacity-75"></span>
              <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-emerald-500"></span>
            </span>
            LIVE LABS
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Center / Bottom Hacker Emblem & Admin-Editable Banner Label */}
        <div className="relative z-10 flex items-center gap-3 pt-2">
          {/* Hacker Badge Shield */}
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-red-600/20 via-red-950/40 to-slate-900 border border-red-500/40 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-red-950/40 group-hover:scale-105 group-hover:border-red-400 transition-all duration-300 shrink-0">
            <Terminal className="w-6 h-6 text-red-400 group-hover:text-red-300 transition-colors" />
          </div>

          {/* Admin-Controlled Banner Text */}

            </div>

              className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-black font-mono tracking-wide text-white uppercase truncate flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5"

              <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-red-500 animate-pulse shrink-0"></span>
              <span className="truncate">{bannerLabel}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Card Content */}
      <div className="p-6 sm:p-7 flex flex-col flex-1 justify-between gap-6 bg-[#090d16]">
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {/* Course Name */}

            {course.title}
          </h3>

          {/* Course Description (Full Visibility - No Line Clamping) */}
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line font-normal">
            {course.summary || course.description}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Structured Meta Cards (Duration & Learning Mode) */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            {/* Duration */}
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800/90 group-hover:border-slate-700 transition-colors flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-red-950/70 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-inner">
                <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                  Duration
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-100 truncate">
                  {durationText}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Learning Mode */}
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800/90 group-hover:border-slate-700 transition-colors flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-red-950/70 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-inner">
                <Laptop className="w-4 h-4" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                  Mode
                </div>
                <div
                  className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-slate-100 truncate"
                  title={learningMode}
                >
                  {learningMode}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Pricing Block: Original Price & Now Price */}
          <div className="p-3.5 sm:p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/90 border border-slate-800/90 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-inner">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                Course Fee
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 mt-0.5">
                {hasDiscount && (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-xs text-slate-400">
                    <span>Original:</span>
                    <span className="text-slate-500 line-through">
                      ₹{originalPrice.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )}
                <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1 font-mono">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-red-400">Now:</span>
                  <span className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
                    ₹{nowPrice.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {discountPercent > 0 ? (
              <span className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-950/80 border border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300 text-xs font-mono font-bold shrink-0 shadow-sm">
                {discountPercent}% OFF
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-950/60 border border-red-800/50 text-red-400 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase shrink-0">
                Live Batch
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Super Apply Now Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleApplyClick}
            className="w-full py-4 px-6 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 via-red-600 to-rose-600 hover:from-red-500 hover:to-rose-500 active:from-red-700 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-lg shadow-red-950/80 hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-red-600/30 transition-all duration-200 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer group/btn relative overflow-hidden"
          >
            {/* Shimmer effect */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 -translate-x-full group-hover/btn:translate-x-full transition-transform duration-1000 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/20 to-transparent pointer-events-none" />

            <span>Apply Now</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 transition-transform group-hover/btn:translate-x-1.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/components/courses/CourseCurriculum.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown, CheckCircle2, Clock, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import { SyllabusModule } from '../../types';

interface CourseCurriculumProps {
  syllabus: SyllabusModule[];
}

export const CourseCurriculum: React.FC<CourseCurriculumProps> = ({ syllabus }) => {
  const [openModule, setOpenModule] = useState<number | null>(syllabus[0]?.module || 1);

  const toggle = (modNum: number) => {
    setOpenModule((prev) => (prev === modNum ? null : modNum));
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2 border-b border-slate-200">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
          <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600" />
          <span>Curriculum Modules ({syllabus.length} Phases)</span>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setOpenModule(openModule ? null : 1)}
          className="text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 hover:text-red-700"
        >
          {openModule ? 'Collapse Active' : 'Expand Phase 1'}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {syllabus.map((mod) => {
          const isOpen = openModule === mod.module;
          return (
            <div
              key={mod.module}
              className={`rounded-xl border transition-all duration-200 overflow-hidden ${
                isOpen
                  ? 'border-red-500/40 bg-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-red-500/10'
                  : 'border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-slate-300'
              }`}
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => toggle(mod.module)}
                className="w-full p-5 text-left flex items-center justify-between gap-4 font-bold text-slate-900"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 min-w-0">
                  <span className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-red-100 text-red-700 font-black text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    {mod.module}
                  </span>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <h4 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-900 truncate">
                      {mod.title}
                    </h4>
                    <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                      <Clock className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" />
                      <span>{mod.duration}</span>
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <ChevronDown
                  className={`w-5 h-5 text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-200 shrink-0 ${
                    isOpen ? 'transform rotate-180 text-red-600' : ''
                  }`}
                />
              </button>

              {isOpen && (
                <div className="px-5 pb-5 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50">
                  <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 mb-3">
                    Hands-on Topics &amp; Range Scenarios:
                  </div>
                  <ul className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">

                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />

import React from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Course, VideoItem } from '../../types';
import { YouTubeEmbed } from '../common/YouTubeEmbed';
import {
  Shield,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  Award,
  Users,
  ArrowRight,
  CheckCircle2,
  PhoneCall,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { trackEvent } from '../../lib/analytics';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';

interface CourseHeroProps {
  course: Course;
  video?: VideoItem;
}

export const CourseHero: React.FC<CourseHeroProps> = ({ course, video }) => {
  const { siteSettings } = useData();

  const handleApplyClick = () => {
    trackEvent('cta_click', course.id, {
      source: 'course_detail_hero_apply',
      course_title: course.title,
    });
  };

  const levelColor = {
    Beginner: 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30',
    Intermediate: 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30',
    Advanced: 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 border-purple-500/30',
    'All Levels': 'bg-slate-500/20 text-slate-300 border-slate-500/30',
  }[course.level] || 'bg-slate-500/20 text-slate-300 border-slate-500/30';

  return (
    <section className="relative bg-slate-950 text-white pt-12 pb-16 lg:pt-16 lg:pb-24 border-b border-slate-800">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10 lg:gap-12 items-center">
          {/* Left Column: Course Details & Sales CTA */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-6">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
              {course.badge_text && (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-white bg-red-600 rounded-md shadow-sm">
                  <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" />
                  <span>{course.badge_text}</span>
                </span>
              )}

              <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded-md border ${levelColor}`}>
                {course.level} Level
              </span>

              {course.seats_remaining != null && course.seats_remaining > 0 ? (
                <span className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded-md bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Users className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>{course.seats_remaining} Seats Remaining</span>
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded-md bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Cohort Admissions Active</span>
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl font-black text-white tracking-tight leading-tight">
              {course.title}
            </h1>

            <p className="text-lg text-red-400 font-semibold leading-snug">
              {course.tagline}
            </p>

            <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-300 leading-relaxed max-w-2xl">
              {course.summary}
            </p>

            {/* Quick Stats Grid */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 pt-2">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
                <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold text-slate-400 block">
                  Duration
                </span>
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                  <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500" />
                  <span>{course.duration_text || `${course.duration_weeks} Weeks`} ({course.hours_per_week}h/w)</span>
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800">
                <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold text-slate-400 block">
                  Next Batch
                </span>
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                  <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500" />
                  <span>
                    {course.batch_start_date
                      ? new Date(course.batch_start_date).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
                          month: 'short',
                          day: 'numeric',
                        })
                      : 'Rolling Intake'}
                  </span>
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
                <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold text-slate-400 block">
                  Program Fee
                </span>
                <span className="text-base font-black text-white mt-0.5 block">
                  ₹{course.fee_amount.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Action Group */}
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-3 pt-2">
              <Link
                to={`/register?course=${course.id}`}
                onClick={handleApplyClick}
                className="w-full sm:w-auto px-8 py-4 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white font-black text-base shadow-xl hover:shadow-brand transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 group cursor-pointer"
              >
                <span>Apply &amp; Reserve Seat</span>
                <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" />
              </Link>

              {siteSettings.contact_whatsapp && (
                <a
                  href={`https://wa.me/${siteSettings.contact_whatsapp.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')}?text=Hi,%20I%20have%20questions%20about%20the%20${encodeURIComponent(course.title)}%20program.`}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="w-full sm:w-auto px-5 py-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 font-semibold text-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  <PhoneCall className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                  <span>Talk to Admissions Lead</span>
                </a>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-400">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
              <span>
                Certification Awarded:{' '}
                <strong className="text-slate-200">{course.certification_title}</strong>
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right Column: Video or Visual Card */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5">
            {video ? (
              <div className="p-2 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 shadow-2xl">
                <YouTubeEmbed
                  urlOrId={video.youtube_video_id || video.youtube_url}
                  title={video.title}
                  description={video.description}
                  autoplay={video.autoplay}
                  muted={video.muted_autoplay}
                  showControls={video.show_controls}
                  courseId={course.id}
                />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 p-8 shadow-2xl space-y-6">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-red-600/20 text-red-500 border border-red-500/30 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Award className="w-6 h-6" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white">Cohort Registration Open</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Strictly 20 Candidates Per Range</p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-3 text-xs text-slate-300">
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 flex items-start gap-2">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <span>Private cloud cyber range instances provisioned for every candidate</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 flex items-start gap-2">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <span>Live adversary emulation and triage scenarios</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 flex items-start gap-2">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <span>Direct recruiter referrals and technical portfolio review</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <Link
                  to={`/register?course=${course.id}`}

File: src/components/home/FaqSection.tsx
