import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown, HelpCircle, MessageCircle, Mail } from 'lucide-react';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';

export const FaqSection: React.FC = () => {
  const { faqs, siteSettings } = useData();
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(faqs[0]?.id || null);

  const activeFaqs = faqs.filter((f) => f.is_active);

  const toggle = (id: string) => {
    setOpenId((prev) => (prev === id ? null : id));
  };

  return (
    <section id="faqs" className="py-20 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-200">
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="text-center mb-12 space-y-3">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-slate-200/80 text-slate-700 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
            <HelpCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-600" />
            <span>Clear Answers &amp; Objection Handling</span>
          </div>

          <h2 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
            Frequently Asked Questions
          </h2>

          <p className="text-slate-600 text-base">
            Everything you need to know about our cyber ranges, admissions criteria, prerequisites, and cohort schedule.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Accordions */}
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {activeFaqs.map((faq) => {
            const isOpen = openId === faq.id;
            return (
              <div
                key={faq.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden transition-all duration-200"
              >
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => toggle(faq.id)}
                  className="w-full px-6 py-4 text-left flex items-center justify-between gap-4 font-bold text-slate-900 text-base hover:text-red-600 transition-colors"
                >
                  <span className="flex-1">{faq.question}</span>
                  <ChevronDown
                    className={`w-5 h-5 text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-200 shrink-0 ${
                      isOpen ? 'transform rotate-180 text-red-600' : ''
                    }`}
                  />
                </button>

                {isOpen && (
                  <div className="px-6 pb-5 pt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50">
                    {faq.answer}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Direct Counselor Help Callout */}
        <div className="mt-10 p-6 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 text-center sm:text-left">
          <div>
            <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Have a specific question about your technical background?</h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Our academic counselors will evaluate your profile and advise you honestly.</p>
          </div>
          {siteSettings.contact_whatsapp ? (
            <a
              href={`https://wa.me/${siteSettings.contact_whatsapp.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')}?text=Hi%20EV%20Cyber%20Academy,%20I%20have%20questions%20regarding%20course%20prerequisites.`}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-semibold text-xs transition flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 shadow-sm"
            >
              <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Ask Academic Counselor</span>
            </a>
          ) : (
            <a
              href={`mailto:${siteSettings.contact_email}?subject=Course%20Prerequisites%20Inquiry`}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 hover:bg-black text-white font-semibold text-xs transition flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 shadow-sm"
            >
              <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />
              <span>Contact Admissions Desk</span>
            </a>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { CourseCard } from '../courses/CourseCard';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { CourseLevel } from '../../types';
import { ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';

export const FeaturedCourses: React.FC = () => {
  const { courses, isLoadingCourses } = useData();
  const [selectedLevel, setSelectedLevel] = useState<string>('All');

  const levels = ['All', 'Beginner', 'Intermediate', 'Advanced'];

  // Published courses only for public showcase
  const publishedCourses = courses.filter((c) => c.status === 'published');

  const filteredCourses =
    selectedLevel === 'All'
      ? publishedCourses
      : publishedCourses.filter((c) => c.level === (selectedLevel as CourseLevel));

  return (
    <section id="programs" className="py-20 bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-6 mb-12">
          <div className="max-w-2xl space-y-3">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-700 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Production-Grade Cybersecurity Programs</span>
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
              Defend, Hunt, or Penetrate: Choose Your Cohort
            </h2>
            <p className="text-slate-600 text-base">
              Every track is 100% hands-on. Live cyber ranges, dedicated cloud labs, real adversary payloads, and zero theoretical filler.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Level Filter Tabs */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 p-1 bg-slate-200/80 rounded-xl self-start md:self-auto">
            {levels.map((lvl) => (
              <button
                key={lvl}
                onClick={() => setSelectedLevel(lvl)}
                className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-all ${
                  selectedLevel === lvl
                    ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                    : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                {lvl === 'All' ? 'All Cohorts' : lvl}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Course Grid */}
        {isLoadingCourses ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => (
              <div key={n} className="h-96 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 animate-pulse p-6" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : filteredCourses.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {filteredCourses.map((course) => (
              <CourseCard key={course.id} course={course} featured={course.featured} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-center py-16 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300">
            <p className="text-slate-500 font-medium">No courses found matching this level.</p>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Bottom Explorer CTA */}
        <div className="mt-12 text-center">
          <Link
            to="/programs"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl transition"
          >
            <span>Compare Full Curriculum & Syllabus Syllabi</span>
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/components/registration/RegistrationForm.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';
import { ExperienceLevel } from '../../types';
import { validateRegistrationForm } from '../../lib/validation';
import { captureUtmParams } from '../../lib/utm';
import { trackEvent } from '../../lib/analytics';
import {
  ShieldCheck,
  User,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  Building,
  GraduationCap,
  Sparkles,
  ArrowRight,
  AlertTriangle,
  Loader2,
  Clock,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface RegistrationFormProps {
  preselectedCourseId?: string;
  onSuccess?: (leadId: string) => void;
}

export const RegistrationForm: React.FC<RegistrationFormProps> = ({
  preselectedCourseId,
  onSuccess,
}) => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const { courses, submitRegistration, checkDuplicateLead } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  const queryCourse = searchParams.get('course') || preselectedCourseId || '';

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    full_name: '',
    email: '',
    phone: '',
    course_id: queryCourse,
    college_company: '',
    experience_level: 'Student / Fresher' as ExperienceLevel,
  });

  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [hasStartedForm, setHasStartedForm] = useState(false);
  const [duplicateWarning, setDuplicateWarning] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Sync course selection if query param changes or preselected prop arrives
  useEffect(() => {
    if (queryCourse && !formData.course_id) {
      setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, course_id: queryCourse }));
    }
  }, [queryCourse, formData.course_id]);

  // Track form start on first interaction
  const handleFormInteraction = () => {
    if (!hasStartedForm) {
      setHasStartedForm(true);
      trackEvent('form_start', formData.course_id);
    }
  };

  const experienceOptions: ExperienceLevel[] = [
    'Student / Fresher',
    '0-2 Years IT/Dev',
    '2-5 Years Tech/Security',
    '5+ Years Professional',
    'Career Switcher',
  ];

  const handleBlurEmailOrCourse = async () => {
    if (formData.email && formData.course_id) {
      const dupCheck = await checkDuplicateLead(formData.email, formData.course_id);
      if (dupCheck.isDuplicate) {
        setDuplicateWarning(dupCheck.message || 'You have already applied for this course.');
      } else {
        setDuplicateWarning(null);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setDuplicateWarning(null);

    // Validate
    const validation = validateRegistrationForm(formData);
    if (!validation.isValid) {
      setErrors(validation.errors);
      notifyError('Please fix the errors indicated in the form.');
      return;
    }

    setErrors({});
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    try {
      // Extract UTM / campaign params automatically
      const utm = captureUtmParams();

      const result = await submitRegistration({
        full_name: formData.full_name,
        email: formData.email,
        phone: formData.phone,
        course_id: formData.course_id,
        college_company: formData.college_company,
        experience_level: formData.experience_level,
        referral_source: utm.ref || 'Direct Web Registration',
        utm_source: utm.utm_source,
        utm_medium: utm.utm_medium,
        utm_campaign: utm.utm_campaign,
        utm_term: utm.utm_term,
        utm_content: utm.utm_content,
      });

      if (!result.success) {
        if (result.isDuplicate) {
          setDuplicateWarning(
            result.error ||
              'An active application for this program is already registered with this email.'
          );
        } else {
          notifyError('Submission Error', result.error || 'Failed to submit registration.');
        }
        setIsSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      success(
        'Registration Recorded!',
        `Your application reference is ${result.lead?.lead_number}. An admissions counselor will reach out shortly.`
      );

      if (onSuccess && result.lead?.id) {
        onSuccess(result.lead.id);
      } else {
        navigate(`/registration-success?leadId=${result.lead?.id}&leadNum=${result.lead?.lead_number}`);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      notifyError('Unexpected error during registration. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const selectedCourse = courses.find((c) => c.id === formData.course_id);

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200/90 shadow-xl overflow-hidden">
      {/* Form Header */}
      <div className="bg-slate-950 text-white p-6 sm:p-8 border-b border-slate-800">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-red-500 mb-2">
          <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>Priority Admissions Application</span>
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
          Reserve Your Cyber Range Seat
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-400 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
          Fill in your details below. No upfront payment required. Our academic admissions lead will verify your prerequisites and schedule your lab walkthrough.
        </p>

        {selectedCourse && (
          <div className="mt-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="font-semibold text-slate-200 truncate">{selectedCourse.title}</span>
            <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold shrink-0 ml-2">
              Fee: ₹{selectedCourse.fee_amount.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
            </span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Duplicate Alert Notice if detected */}
      {duplicateWarning && (
        <div className="m-6 p-4 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-900 flex items-start gap-3">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="text-xs space-y-1">
            <p className="font-bold">Existing Application On Record</p>
            <p className="leading-relaxed text-amber-800">{duplicateWarning}</p>
            <p className="text-amber-700 font-medium pt-1">
              Need urgent assistance? Chat with an admissions lead directly on WhatsApp.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Form Fields */}
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} onFocus={handleFormInteraction} className="p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
        {/* Full Name */}
        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
            Full Name <span className="text-red-600">*</span>
          </label>
          <div className="relative">
            <User className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              placeholder="e.g. Rahul Sharma"
              value={formData.full_name}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, full_name: e.target.value })}
              className={`w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-xl border bg-slate-50/50 focus:bg-white transition focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 ${
                errors.full_name
                  ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500/20'
                  : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-red-600 focus:ring-red-600/10'
              }`}
            />
          </div>
          {errors.full_name && (
            <p className="text-xs text-red-600 mt-1 font-medium">{errors.full_name}</p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Email & Phone in 2 cols */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Email Address <span className="text-red-600">*</span>
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
              <input
                type="email"
                required
                placeholder="you@company.com"
                value={formData.email}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, email: e.target.value })}
                onBlur={handleBlurEmailOrCourse}
                className={`w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-xl border bg-slate-50/50 focus:bg-white transition focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 ${
                  errors.email
                    ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500/20'
                    : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-red-600 focus:ring-red-600/10'
                }`}
              />
            </div>
            {errors.email && (
              <p className="text-xs text-red-600 mt-1 font-medium">{errors.email}</p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              WhatsApp / Mobile Number <span className="text-red-600">*</span>
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Phone className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
              <input
                type="tel"
                required
                placeholder="+91 98765 43210"
                value={formData.phone}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, phone: e.target.value })}
                className={`w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-xl border bg-slate-50/50 focus:bg-white transition focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 ${
                  errors.phone
                    ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500/20'
                    : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-red-600 focus:ring-red-600/10'
                }`}
              />
            </div>
            {errors.phone && (
              <p className="text-xs text-red-600 mt-1 font-medium">{errors.phone}</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Course Selection */}
        <div>
          <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
            Select Cybersecurity Program <span className="text-red-600">*</span>
          </label>
          <select
            required
            value={formData.course_id}
            onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, course_id: e.target.value })}
            onBlur={handleBlurEmailOrCourse}
            className={`w-full px-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-xl border bg-slate-50/50 focus:bg-white transition focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 ${
              errors.course_id
                ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500/20'
                : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-red-600 focus:ring-red-600/10'
            }`}
          >
            <option value="">-- Choose Course Cohort --</option>
            {courses.map((course) => (
              <option key={course.id} value={course.id}>
                {course.title} ({course.level} &bull; {course.duration_text || `${course.duration_weeks} Weeks`} &bull; ₹
                {course.fee_amount.toLocaleString('en-IN')})
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {errors.course_id && (
            <p className="text-xs text-red-600 mt-1 font-medium">{errors.course_id}</p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* College / Organization & Experience Level in 2 cols */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">

            <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              College or Company Name <span className="text-red-600">*</span>

                type="text"

            )}

            <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Technical Experience Level <span className="text-red-600">*</span>
            </label>

              <GraduationCap className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
              <select
                value={formData.experience_level}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setFormData({ ...formData, experience_level: e.target.value as ExperienceLevel })
                }
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-slate-50/50 focus:bg-white transition focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:border-red-600 focus:ring-red-600/10"
              >
                {experienceOptions.map((opt) => (
                  <option key={opt} value={opt}>
                    {opt}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Submit Button */}
        <div className="pt-2">
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            className="w-full py-4 px-6 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 disabled:opacity-50 text-white font-black text-base shadow-xl hover:shadow-brand transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 group cursor-pointer"
          >
            {isSubmitting ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />
                <span>Submitting Your Application...</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <span>Complete Free Application &amp; Reserve Seat</span>
                <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" />
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="text-center space-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
          <p className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
            <span>Admissions counselor callback scheduled within 24 hours of submission.</span>
          </p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            Strictly zero third-party spam. Your contact details are protected under EV privacy standards.
          </p>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
};

File: src/components/registration/SimpleRegistrationModal.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, CheckCircle2, User, Mail, Phone, ArrowRight, Loader2, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import { Course } from '../../types';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';

interface SimpleRegistrationModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  selectedCourse: Course | null;
}

export const SimpleRegistrationModal: React.FC<SimpleRegistrationModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  selectedCourse,
}) => {
  const { courses, submitRegistration } = useData();

  const [courseId, setCourseId] = useState<string>(selectedCourse?.id || '');
  const [studentName, setStudentName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [contactNumber, setContactNumber] = useState('');

  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submittedLeadNumber, setSubmittedLeadNumber] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Sync selected course when modal opens
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedCourse) {
      setCourseId(selectedCourse.id);
    } else if (courses.length > 0 && !courseId) {
      setCourseId(courses[0].id);
    }
  }, [selectedCourse, courses, courseId]);

  // Handle Escape key to close modal
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        setStudentName('');
        setEmail('');
        setContactNumber('');
        setErrorMessage(null);
        setSubmittedLeadNumber(null);
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const currentCourse = courses.find((c) => c.id === courseId) || selectedCourse;

  const handleResetAndClose = () => {
    setStudentName('');
    setEmail('');
    setContactNumber('');
    setErrorMessage(null);
    setSubmittedLeadNumber(null);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMessage(null);

    if (!studentName.trim() || !email.trim() || !contactNumber.trim()) {
      setErrorMessage('Please fill in your name, email address, and contact number.');
      return;
    }

    if (!courseId) {
      setErrorMessage('Please select a course to register.');
      return;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);

    const result = await submitRegistration({
      full_name: studentName.trim(),
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      phone: contactNumber.trim(),
      course_id: courseId,
      college_company: 'Not Specified',
      experience_level: 'Student / Fresher',
      referral_source: 'Website Course Page',
    });

    setIsSubmitting(false);

    if (result.success && result.lead) {
      setSubmittedLeadNumber(result.lead.lead_number);
    } else {
      setErrorMessage(
        result.error || 'Failed to submit registration. Please try again or contact us directly.'
      );
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      onClick={handleResetAndClose}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6"
    >
      <div
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className="relative w-full max-w-lg bg-[#090d16] rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-800 text-white overflow-hidden animate-fade-in"
      >
        {/* Ambient Red Glow in corner */}
        <div className="absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-40 h-40 bg-red-600/15 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Close Button (z-50 ensure it is always above all modal content and clickable) */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            handleResetAndClose();
          }}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 sm:top-5 sm:right-5 z-50 p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800/90 rounded-full transition cursor-pointer"
          aria-label="Close form"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5 pointer-events-none" />
        </button>

        {submittedLeadNumber ? (
          /* Success Screen */
          <div className="p-8 sm:p-10 text-center space-y-6 relative z-10">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-400 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-950/50">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-9 h-9" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3">
              <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
                Registration Successful
              </h3>

              <div className="inline-block px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-950/60 border border-red-800/60 text-red-400 text-xs font-mono font-bold tracking-wider">
                Application Ref: {submittedLeadNumber}
              </div>

              <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-300 max-w-md mx-auto leading-relaxed pt-2 font-normal">
                Thank you for registering your interest with EV Cyber Academy. Our team will contact you shortly.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-800/80">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleResetAndClose}
                className="w-full py-3 px-6 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white font-bold text-sm transition cursor-pointer border border-slate-700"
              >
                Close
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Registration Form */
          <div className="p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6 relative z-10">
            <div>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-red-500">
                EV Cyber Academy Private Limited
              </span>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tight mt-0.5">
                Course Registration
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">
                Enter your details to register your application.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Selected Course Display */}
            {currentCourse && (
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-red-950/60 border border-red-900/40 text-red-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                      Selected Course
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate">
                      {currentCourse.title}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {courses.length > 1 && (
                  <select
                    value={courseId}
                    onChange={(e) => setCourseId(e.target.value)}
                    className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 focus:outline-none focus:border-red-500 shrink-0"
                  >
                    {courses.map((c) => (
                      <option key={c.id} value={c.id} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                        {c.title}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {errorMessage && (
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-red-950/60 border border-red-500/40 text-red-300 text-xs leading-relaxed">
                {errorMessage}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Simple Form (Only 3 inputs) */}
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              {/* 1. Student Name */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5 font-mono">
                  Student Name <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <User className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    placeholder="Enter your full name"
                    value={studentName}
                    onChange={(e) => setStudentName(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-white placeholder-slate-500 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/30 focus:border-red-500 transition"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* 2. Email Address */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5 font-mono">
                  Email Address <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    placeholder="name@example.com"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-white placeholder-slate-500 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/30 focus:border-red-500 transition"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* 3. Contact Number */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5 font-mono">
                  Contact Number <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Phone className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    type="tel"
                    required
                    placeholder="e.g. +91 98765 43210"
                    value={contactNumber}
                    onChange={(e) => setContactNumber(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-white placeholder-slate-500 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/30 focus:border-red-500 transition"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Submit Button */}
              <div className="pt-2">
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={isSubmitting}
                  className="w-full py-3.5 px-6 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 via-red-600 to-rose-600 hover:from-red-500 hover:to-rose-500 active:from-red-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-lg shadow-red-950/50 hover:shadow-red-600/30 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {isSubmitting ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                      <span>Submitting Registration...</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <span>Submit Registration</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>

              <p className="text-[11px] text-center text-slate-500 pt-1 font-mono">
                Your application will be sent directly to the EV Cyber Academy admissions desk.
              </p>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/context/AuthContext.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { UserProfile } from '../types';
import { supabase, isSupabaseConfigured } from '../lib/supabase';
import { INITIAL_STAFF } from '../lib/mockData';

interface AuthContextType {
  user: UserProfile | null;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  isSuperAdmin: boolean;
  isLoading: boolean;
  signIn: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
  staffList: UserProfile[];
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const LOCAL_AUTH_KEY = 'ev_active_user_session';

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [staffList, setStaffList] = useState<UserProfile[]>(() => isSupabaseConfigured() ? [] : INITIAL_STAFF);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function initAuth() {
      setIsLoading(true);

      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
        try {
          const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
          if (session?.user) {
            const { data: profile } = await supabase
              .from('profiles')
              .select('*')
              .eq('id', session.user.id)
              .single();

            if (profile) {
              setUser(profile as UserProfile);
            } else {
              setUser({
                id: session.user.id,
                email: session.user.email || '',
                full_name: session.user.user_metadata?.full_name || 'Staff User',
                role: (session.user.user_metadata?.role as 'super_admin' | 'staff') || 'staff',
                is_active: true,
              });
            }
          }

          // Fetch active staff for assignments
          const { data: staffMembers } = await supabase
            .from('profiles')
            .select('*')
            .eq('is_active', true);

          if (staffMembers && staffMembers.length > 0) {
            setStaffList(staffMembers as UserProfile[]);
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Supabase auth initialization error:', err);
        }
      } else {
        const stored = localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_AUTH_KEY);
        if (stored) {
          try {
            setUser(JSON.parse(stored));
          } catch {
            localStorage.removeItem(LOCAL_AUTH_KEY);
          }
        }
      }

      setIsLoading(false);
    }

    initAuth();

    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(async (_event, session) => {
        if (session?.user) {
          const { data: profile } = await supabase
            .from('profiles')
            .select('*')
            .eq('id', session.user.id)
            .single();

          if (profile) {
            setUser(profile as UserProfile);
          } else {
            setUser({
              id: session.user.id,
              email: session.user.email || '',
              full_name: session.user.user_metadata?.full_name || 'Staff User',
              role: (session.user.user_metadata?.role as 'super_admin' | 'staff') || 'staff',
              is_active: true,
            });
          }
        } else {
          setUser(null);
        }
      });

      return () => {
        subscription.unsubscribe();
      };
    }
  }, []);

  const signIn = async (email: string, password: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> => {
    setIsLoading(true);

    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
          email: email.trim(),
          password,
        });

        if (error) {
          setIsLoading(false);
          return { success: false, error: error.message };
        }

        if (data.user) {
          const { data: profile } = await supabase
            .from('profiles')
            .select('*')
            .eq('id', data.user.id)
            .single();

          if (profile) {
            setUser(profile as UserProfile);
          } else {
            setUser({
              id: data.user.id,
              email: data.user.email || '',
              full_name: data.user.user_metadata?.full_name || 'Staff User',
              role: (data.user.user_metadata?.role as 'super_admin' | 'staff') || 'staff',
              is_active: true,
            });
          }
        }

        setIsLoading(false);
        return { success: true };
      } catch (err) {
        setIsLoading(false);
        return { success: false, error: (err as Error).message };
      }
    } else {
      // Local development fallback
      const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
      const matched = INITIAL_STAFF.find(
        (s) => s.email.toLowerCase() === cleanEmail
      );

      if (matched) {
        setUser(matched);
        localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_AUTH_KEY, JSON.stringify(matched));
        setIsLoading(false);
        return { success: true };
      }

      setIsLoading(false);
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'Invalid credentials. Please contact your system administrator.',
      };
    }
  };

  const signOut = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      await supabase.auth.signOut();
    }
    setUser(null);
    localStorage.removeItem(LOCAL_AUTH_KEY);
  };

  const value = {
    user,
    isAuthenticated: Boolean(user),
    isSuperAdmin: user?.role === 'super_admin',
    isLoading,
    signIn,
    signOut,
    staffList,
  };

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
};

export const useAuth = (): AuthContextType => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};
