  Lock,
} from 'lucide-react';

export const CampaignCoursePage: React.FC = () => {
  const { slug } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();
  const { courses, isLoadingCourses, getCourseBySlug, siteSettings } = useData();
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);

  // Look up matching course
  const targetCourse = slug ? getCourseBySlug(slug) : undefined;
  const course = targetCourse && targetCourse.status === 'published' ? targetCourse : undefined;

  // Auto-open registration modal on load if configured by admin
  useEffect(() => {
    if (course) {
      trackEvent('page_view', course.id, {
        page: 'campaign_landing_page',
        slug,
        course_title: course.title,
      });

      if (course.auto_open_modal) {
        setIsModalOpen(true);
      }
    }
  }, [course?.id, course?.auto_open_modal, slug]);

  // Loading state: While courses are loading or list is empty on mount, show clean cyber loader
  if (isLoadingCourses || (courses.length === 0 && !course)) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[75vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center space-y-4">
        <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-red-600/10 border border-red-500/30 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-red-950/50">
          <Loader2 className="w-7 h-7 text-red-500 animate-spin" />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <p className="text-base font-bold text-white tracking-wide">Loading Program...</p>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">EV Cyber Academy &bull; Direct Enrollment Track</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // If loading finished and course is not found, cleanly redirect to home
  if (!course) {
    return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  }

  // Course attributes
  const durationText =
    course.duration_text ||
    (course.duration_weeks ? `${course.duration_weeks} Weeks` : 'Self-Paced');
  const learningMode =
    course.learning_mode || course.tagline || 'Live Online & Hands-on Cyber Labs';
  const bannerLabel =
    course.badge_text ||
    siteSettings.default_card_banner_text ||
    'Professional Certification Track';

  const nowPrice = course.fee_amount ?? 0;
  const originalPrice = course.original_price ?? 0;
  const hasDiscount = originalPrice > nowPrice && originalPrice > 0;
  const discountPercent = hasDiscount
    ? Math.round(((originalPrice - nowPrice) / originalPrice) * 100)
    : 0;

  const handleApplyClick = () => {
    setIsModalOpen(true);
    trackEvent('cta_click', course.id, {
      course_name: course.title,
      source: 'campaign_page_apply_now',
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[85vh] py-8 sm:py-14 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-center">
      {/* Cyber Grid Background Matrix & Red Ambient Glows */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,#ef444408_1px,transparent_1px),linear-gradient(to_bottom,#ef444408_1px,transparent_1px)] bg-[size:2rem_2rem] pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute top-1/4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[600px] h-[300px] bg-red-600/15 blur-[120px] rounded-full pointer-events-none" />

      <div className="relative z-10 max-w-3xl mx-auto w-full space-y-6">
        {/* Top Minimal Trust Badge */}
        <div className="text-center space-y-2">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-red-950/70 border border-red-800/60 text-red-400 text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider backdrop-blur-sm shadow-inner">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />
            <span>EV Cyber Academy Private Limited &bull; Direct Campaign Enrollment</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Focused Single-Course Hero Card */}
        <div className="bg-[#090d16] rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 shadow-2xl hover:border-red-500/40 transition-all duration-300 overflow-hidden">
          {/* Top Accent Gradient Bar */}
          <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 via-rose-500 to-amber-500" />

          {/* Banner Badge */}
          <div className="px-6 sm:px-8 pt-6">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-950/60 border border-red-800/50 text-red-400 text-xs font-mono font-bold">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-400 shrink-0" />
              <span>{bannerLabel}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Course Details Content */}
          <div className="p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
            {/* Title & Tagline */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-white tracking-tight leading-snug">
                {course.title}
              </h1>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-400">
                {learningMode}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Full Course Description (Uncut) */}
            <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800/90">
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 leading-relaxed font-normal whitespace-pre-line">
                {course.description || course.summary}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Quick Badges Row (Duration & Learning Mode) */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800 text-slate-300">
                <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500 shrink-0" />
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-500 block">Program Duration</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-white text-xs">{durationText}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800 text-slate-300">
                <Laptop className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500 shrink-0" />
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-500 block">Training Format</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-white text-xs">{learningMode}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Pricing Card & Immediate CTA Button */}
            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 flex-wrap">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block">
                    Special Program Fee
                  </span>
                  <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2.5 mt-0.5">
                    <span className="font-mono text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
                      ₹{nowPrice.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                    </span>
                    {hasDiscount && (
                      <span className="font-mono text-base sm:text-lg text-slate-500 line-through">
                        ₹{originalPrice.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {hasDiscount && (
                  <span className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-950/80 border border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-400 text-xs font-mono font-bold">
                    {discountPercent}% OFF ACTIVE
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Primary Apply Now CTA Button */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleApplyClick}
                className="w-full py-4 px-6 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 via-red-600 to-rose-600 hover:from-red-500 hover:to-rose-500 active:from-red-700 text-white font-bold text-base shadow-xl shadow-red-950/80 hover:shadow-2xl hover:shadow-red-600/30 transition-all duration-200 flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer relative overflow-hidden group/btn"
              >
                <span>Apply Now for {course.title}</span>
                <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5 group-hover/btn:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 -translate-x-full group-hover/btn:translate-x-full transition-transform duration-1000 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/20 to-transparent pointer-events-none" />
              </button>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400 text-center">
                <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
                <span>Instant Admission Registration &bull; EV Cyber Academy Verified</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Subtle Back to All Courses link */}
        <div className="text-center pt-2">
          <Link
            to="/"
            className="text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"
          >
            <span>Want to explore all programs?</span>
            <span className="text-red-400 font-bold underline underline-offset-2">Browse All Courses &rarr;</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Registration Modal Pre-Selected with This Course */}
      <SimpleRegistrationModal
        isOpen={isModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
        selectedCourse={course}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams, Link, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useData } from '../context/DataContext';
import { CourseHero } from '../components/courses/CourseHero';
import { CourseCurriculum } from '../components/courses/CourseCurriculum';
import { CourseKeyHighlights } from '../components/courses/CourseKeyHighlights';
import { StickyCtaBar } from '../components/common/StickyCtaBar';
import { TestimonialsSection } from '../components/home/TestimonialsSection';
import { FaqSection } from '../components/home/FaqSection';
import { RegistrationForm } from '../components/registration/RegistrationForm';
import { trackEvent } from '../lib/analytics';
import {
  ShieldCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  Award,
  Users,
  PhoneCall,
  ArrowRight,
  MessageCircle,
  Mail,
} from 'lucide-react';

export const CourseDetailPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { slug } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();
  const { getCourseBySlug, getCourseVideo, siteSettings } = useData();

  const course = slug ? getCourseBySlug(slug) : undefined;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (course) {
      trackEvent('course_view', course.id, {
        slug: course.slug,
        title: course.title,
      });
      window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    }
  }, [course]);

  if (!course) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[70vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 mb-2">Program Not Found</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-6">
          The requested course URL does not exist or may have been updated.
        </p>
        <Link
          to="/programs"
          className="px-6 py-3 rounded-xl bg-red-600 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow hover:bg-red-700 transition"
        >
          Browse All Available Programs
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const promoVideo = getCourseVideo(course.id);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50">
      {/* Course Hero Section */}
      <CourseHero course={course} video={promoVideo} />

      {/* Main Content Layout */}
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-16">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10 lg:gap-12 items-start">
          {/* Left Column: Curriculum & Learning Outcomes */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-12">
            {/* Detailed Course Overview */}
            {course.description && (
              <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-xs space-y-4">
                <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
                  Program Architecture &amp; Methodology
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                  {course.description}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Curriculum Accordion Breakdown */}
            <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-xs space-y-6">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
                  Comprehensive Syllabus &amp; Lab Schedule
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                  100% hands-on. Every single phase includes enterprise cyber range drills.
                </p>
              </div>

              <CourseCurriculum syllabus={course.syllabus} />
            </div>

            {/* Target Capabilities, Tools & Prerequisites */}
            <CourseKeyHighlights course={course} />
          </div>

          {/* Right Column: Sticky Embedded Application Card & Counselor Help */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 sticky top-28 space-y-6">
            {/* Embedded Registration Form */}
            <div id="apply-card">
              <RegistrationForm preselectedCourseId={course.id} />
            </div>

            {/* Direct Admissions Helpline Box */}
            <div className="p-6 rounded-3xl bg-slate-950 text-white border border-slate-800 shadow-xl space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-red-600/20 text-red-500 border border-red-500/30 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <PhoneCall className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Need Pre-Enrollment Guidance?</h4>

                {siteSettings.contact_whatsapp && (
                  <a
                    href={`https://wa.me/${siteSettings.contact_whatsapp.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')}?text=Hi%20EV%20Cyber%20Academy,%20I%20have%20questions%20regarding%20${encodeURIComponent(course.title)}.`}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                    className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold text-xs transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow"
                  >
                    <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>Chat on WhatsApp</span>
                  </a>
                )}

                {siteSettings.contact_phone ? (
                  <a
                    href={`tel:${siteSettings.contact_phone}`}
                    className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 font-semibold text-xs transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                  >

                    <span>Call Admissions Lead ({siteSettings.contact_phone})</span>
                  </a>
                ) : (
                  <a
                    href={`mailto:${siteSettings.contact_email}?subject=Inquiry%20regarding%20${encodeURIComponent(course.title)}`}
                    className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 font-semibold text-xs transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                  >
                    <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500" />
                    <span>Email Admissions Desk</span>
                  </a>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Alumni Testimonials */}
      <TestimonialsSection />

      {/* Objection Handling FAQs */}
      <FaqSection />

      {/* Sticky Bottom CTA Bar */}
      <StickyCtaBar course={course} />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useData } from '../context/DataContext';
import { CourseCard } from '../components/courses/CourseCard';
import { SimpleRegistrationModal } from '../components/registration/SimpleRegistrationModal';
import { YouTubeEmbed } from '../components/common/YouTubeEmbed';
import { Course } from '../types';
import {
  ShieldCheck,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  MapPin,
  Sparkles,
  ArrowRight,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { trackEvent } from '../lib/analytics';

export const HomePage: React.FC = () => {
  const { courses, videos, siteSettings, getHeroVideo } = useData();

  const [selectedCourse, setSelectedCourse] = useState<Course | null>(null);
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    trackEvent('page_view', undefined, { page: 'home_single_page' });
  }, []);

  const handleApply = (course: Course) => {
    setSelectedCourse(course);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
    trackEvent('cta_click', course.id, {
      course_name: course.title,
      source: 'course_card_apply',
    });
  };

  const handleGeneralApply = () => {
    if (courses.length > 0) {
      setSelectedCourse(courses[0]);
    } else {
      setSelectedCourse(null);
    }
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleQuickApply1 = () => {
    let target = siteSettings.quick_btn1_course_id
      ? courses.find((c) => c.id === siteSettings.quick_btn1_course_id)
      : undefined;

    if (!target) {
      target = courses.find(
        (c) =>
          c.title.toLowerCase().includes('14-day') ||
          c.title.toLowerCase().includes('14 day') ||
          c.title.toLowerCase().includes('499')
      );
    }

    if (!target) {
      target = courses[0] || {
        id: siteSettings.quick_btn1_course_id || 'e5f6a7b8-c9d0-1e2f-3a4b-5c6d7e8f9012',
        title: siteSettings.quick_btn1_course_name || '14-Day Ethical Hacking Program',
        slug: '14-days-ethical-hacking-program',
        tagline: '14 Days Intensive Ethical Hacking & Hands-on Cyber Training',
        summary:
          'A fast-paced, practical 14-day crash program covering cybersecurity fundamentals, network reconnaissance, web penetration, and defense techniques.',
        level: 'Beginner',
        duration_weeks: 2,
        duration_text: '14 Days',
        learning_mode: 'Live Daily Sessions & Hands-on Labs',
        hours_per_week: 10,
        fee_amount: 499,
        currency: 'INR',
        status: 'published',
        featured: true,
        certification_title: 'EV Certified Junior Ethical Hacker',
      };
    }

    setSelectedCourse(target);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
    trackEvent('cta_click', target.id, {
      course_name: target.title,
      source: 'quick_apply_btn_1',
    });
  };

  const handleQuickApply2 = () => {
    let target = siteSettings.quick_btn2_course_id
      ? courses.find((c) => c.id === siteSettings.quick_btn2_course_id)
      : undefined;

    if (!target) {
      target = courses.find(
        (c) =>
          c.title.toLowerCase().includes('offensive') ||
          c.title.toLowerCase().includes('full ethical') ||
          c.title.toLowerCase().includes('red team')
      );
    }

    if (!target) {
      target = (courses.length > 1 ? courses[1] : courses[0]) || {
        id: siteSettings.quick_btn2_course_id || 'b2c3d4e5-f6a7-8b9c-0d1e-2f3a4b5c6d7e',
        title: siteSettings.quick_btn2_course_name || 'Full Ethical Hacking Course',
        slug: 'full-ethical-hacking-course',
        tagline: 'Learn Real-world Adversary Emulation, Active Directory Exploitation & Evasion',
        summary:
          'Comprehensive offensive security and red teaming masterclass from network penetration to Active Directory exploitation.',
        level: 'Advanced',
        duration_weeks: 16,
        duration_text: '16 Weeks',
        hours_per_week: 12,
        fee_amount: 64999,
        currency: 'INR',
        status: 'published',
        featured: true,
        certification_title: 'EV Certified Red Team Specialist',
      };
    }

    setSelectedCourse(target);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
    trackEvent('cta_click', target.id, {
      course_name: target.title,
      source: 'quick_apply_btn_2',
    });
  };

  const scrollToSection = (id: string) => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  // Get active courses sorted strictly by admin display order (1, 2, 3...)
  const publishedCourses = courses.filter((c) => c.status === 'published');
  const baseCourses = publishedCourses.length > 0 ? publishedCourses : courses;
  const displayCourses = [...baseCourses].sort(
    (a, b) => (a.sort_order ?? 999) - (b.sort_order ?? 999)
  );

  // Get introduction / promotional video
  const heroVideo = getHeroVideo() || (videos.length > 0 ? videos[0] : null);
  const videoUrl = heroVideo?.youtube_video_id || heroVideo?.youtube_url || 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=inWWhr5tnEA';

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#030712] flex flex-col text-slate-100">
      {/* =========================================================================
          1. COURSES & PROGRAMS – FIRST SECTION (IMMEDIATE COURSE CARDS)
      ========================================================================== */}
      <section id="courses" className="pt-8 pb-16 sm:pt-12 sm:pb-20 bg-[#030712] border-b border-slate-800/80 scroll-mt-20 flex-1 relative overflow-hidden">
        {/* Subtle Cyber Grid Matrix Background & Glow */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,#ef444408_1px,transparent_1px),linear-gradient(to_bottom,#ef444408_1px,transparent_1px)] bg-[size:2rem_2rem] pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[700px] h-[300px] bg-red-600/10 blur-[120px] rounded-full pointer-events-none" />

        <div className="relative z-10 max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-8 sm:space-y-10">
          {/* Top Brand Identity & 2 Quick Apply Action Buttons (Admin Editable) */}
          <div className="text-center max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-5">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-red-950/60 border border-red-800/50 text-red-400 text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider backdrop-blur-sm shadow-inner">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />
              <span>EV Cyber Academy Private Limited</span>
            </div>

            {/* 2 Quick Apply Buttons */}
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-4 pt-1">
              {siteSettings.quick_btn1_enabled !== false && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleQuickApply1}
                  className="w-full sm:w-auto px-7 py-4 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 via-red-600 to-rose-600 hover:from-red-500 hover:to-rose-500 active:from-red-700 text-white font-black text-sm sm:text-base shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-red-600/30 hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition-all duration-200 flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer relative overflow-hidden group/btn"
                >
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 -translate-x-full group-hover/btn:translate-x-full transition-transform duration-1000 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/20 to-transparent pointer-events-none" />
                  <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-300 shrink-0" />
                  <span>
                    {siteSettings.quick_btn1_text ||
                      'Apply for ₹499 - 14 Days Ethical Hacking Program'}
                  </span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 group-hover/btn:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
                </button>
              )}

              {siteSettings.quick_btn2_enabled !== false && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleQuickApply2}
                  className="w-full sm:w-auto px-7 py-4 rounded-2xl bg-[#090d16] hover:bg-[#111726] active:bg-black text-slate-200 font-bold text-sm sm:text-base shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-slate-900/40 hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition-all duration-200 flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer relative overflow-hidden border border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700 group/btn2"
                >
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 -translate-x-full group-hover/btn2:translate-x-full transition-transform duration-1000 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/10 to-transparent pointer-events-none" />
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-red-500 shrink-0" />
                  <span>
                    {siteSettings.quick_btn2_text || 'Apply to Full Ethical Hacking Course'}
                  </span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 group-hover/btn2:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Course Cards Grid - First thing student sees */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
            {displayCourses.map((course) => (
              <CourseCard key={course.id} course={course} onApply={handleApply} />
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Quick General Application Banner */}
          <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl bg-[#090d16] text-white border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-slate-700/80 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-6 shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden">
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600/5 via-transparent to-transparent pointer-events-none" />
            <div className="space-y-1 text-center sm:text-left relative z-10">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">
                Not sure which course is right for your background?
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Submit a general registration and our admissions team will advise you directly.
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleGeneralApply}
              className="px-6 py-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 to-rose-600 hover:from-red-500 hover:to-rose-500 active:from-red-700 text-white font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider transition shrink-0 cursor-pointer shadow-lg shadow-red-950/50 relative z-10"
            >
              General Application
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =========================================================================
          2. INTRODUCTION / PROMOTIONAL VIDEO (ADMIN TOGGLEABLE ON/OFF)
      ========================================================================== */}
      {siteSettings.show_intro_video !== false && (
        <section id="overview" className="py-14 sm:py-20 bg-[#060a14] border-b border-slate-800/80 scroll-mt-20 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(to_right,#ef444405_1px,transparent_1px),linear-gradient(to_bottom,#ef444405_1px,transparent_1px)] bg-[size:2rem_2rem] pointer-events-none" />
          <div className="relative z-10 max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-6">
            <div className="text-center space-y-2">
              <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-red-500">
                Overview
              </span>
              <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
                Introduction to EV Cyber Academy
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-400 max-w-xl mx-auto">
                Get an inside look at EV Cyber Academy&apos;s practical training methodology, cyber range labs, and technical programs.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-2 sm:p-3 rounded-3xl bg-[#090d16] border border-slate-800/90 shadow-2xl ring-1 ring-slate-800">
              <YouTubeEmbed
                urlOrId={videoUrl}
                title={heroVideo?.title || 'EV Cyber Academy Overview'}
                description={heroVideo?.description || 'Introduction to practical cybersecurity training at EV Cyber Academy'}
                autoplay={false}
                muted={true}
                showControls={true}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* =========================================================================
          3. SIMPLE CONTACT INFORMATION
      ========================================================================== */}
      <section id="contact" className="py-14 sm:py-20 bg-[#030712] scroll-mt-20 relative">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-10">
          <div className="text-center space-y-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-red-500">
              Get in Touch
            </span>
            <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
              Contact for Courses
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-400 max-w-xl mx-auto">
              Have questions regarding our training programs or batch schedules? Contact EV Cyber Academy Private Limited.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-6 text-center">
            {/* Phone / Helpline */}
            <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-[#090d16] border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-red-500/40 shadow-xl transition-all duration-300 space-y-3 group">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-red-950/40 border border-red-900/40 text-red-500 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                <Phone className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-white text-sm">Contact Number</h4>
              {siteSettings.contact_phone ? (
                <a
                  href={`tel:${siteSettings.contact_phone}`}
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-red-400 transition block"
                >
                  {siteSettings.contact_phone}
                </a>
              ) : (
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Contact through official email</p>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Email */}
            <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-[#090d16] border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-red-500/40 shadow-xl transition-all duration-300 space-y-3 group">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-red-950/40 border border-red-900/40 text-red-500 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                <Mail className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-white text-sm">Official Email</h4>
              <a
                href={`mailto:${siteSettings.contact_email}`}
                className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-red-400 transition block truncate"
              >
                {siteSettings.contact_email}
              </a>
            </div>

            {/* Office Location */}
            <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-[#090d16] border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-red-500/40 shadow-xl transition-all duration-300 space-y-3 group">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-red-950/40 border border-red-900/40 text-red-500 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                <MapPin className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-white text-sm">Location</h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                {siteSettings.contact_address || 'Bangalore & Online Worldwide'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =========================================================================
          5. SIMPLE REGISTRATION MODAL
      ========================================================================== */}
      <SimpleRegistrationModal
        isOpen={isModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
        selectedCourse={selectedCourse}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/pages/LoginPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useData } from '../context/DataContext';
import { Logo } from '../components/common/Logo';
import { Lock, Mail, ArrowRight, AlertCircle, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNotification } from '../context/NotificationContext';

export const LoginPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { signIn, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
  const { fetchLeads } = useData();
  const { error: notifyError, success } = useNotification();

  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // If already logged in, redirect to /admin
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (isAuthenticated) {
      navigate('/admin');
    }
  }, [isAuthenticated, navigate]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMessage(null);
    setIsLoading(true);

    const result = await signIn(email, password);

    if (result.success) {
      // Hydrate leads directly into memory before navigating
      await fetchLeads();
      setIsLoading(false);
      success('Logged in successfully.');
      navigate('/admin');
    } else {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setErrorMessage(result.error || 'Authentication failed. Please verify credentials.');
      notifyError('Login failed', result.error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-white flex flex-col justify-center py-12 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 relative overflow-hidden">
      {/* Background accents */}
      <div className="absolute top-1/4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-96 h-96 bg-red-600/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

      <div className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md relative z-10 text-center">
        <Link to="/" className="inline-block focus:outline-none">
          <Logo variant="light" size="lg" />
        </Link>
        <h2 className="mt-6 text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
          Admin Panel Login
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-400">
          EV Cyber Academy Private Limited &bull; Course Registration Management
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8 sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md relative z-10">
        <div className="bg-slate-900 py-8 px-6 shadow-2xl rounded-3xl sm:px-10 border border-slate-800 space-y-6">
          {errorMessage && (
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-red-950/60 border border-red-500/30 text-red-300 text-xs flex items-start gap-2.5">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{errorMessage}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Login Form */}
          <form className="space-y-4" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300 mb-1">
                Corporate Email
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  placeholder="name@evcyberacademy.com"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-600/30 focus:border-red-600"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300 mb-1">
                Password
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  placeholder="••••••••••••"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-white text-xs focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-600/30 focus:border-red-600"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="pt-2">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isLoading}
                className="w-full py-3.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 disabled:opacity-50 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-lg transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 group cursor-pointer"
              >
                {isLoading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                    <span>Signing in...</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <span>Sign In to Admin Panel</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" />
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>

          <div className="text-center pt-2 border-t border-slate-800">
            <Link
              to="/"
              className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition"
            >
              &larr; Return to EV Cyber Academy Public Site
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/pages/ProgramsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useData } from '../context/DataContext';
import { CourseCard } from '../components/courses/CourseCard';
import { CourseLevel } from '../types';
import { Search, Shield, Filter, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { trackEvent } from '../lib/analytics';

export const ProgramsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { courses, isLoadingCourses } = useData();
  const [selectedLevel, setSelectedLevel] = useState<string>('All');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');

  useEffect(() => {
    trackEvent('page_view', undefined, { page: 'programs_listing' });
  }, []);

  const levels = ['All', 'Beginner', 'Intermediate', 'Advanced'];
  const publishedCourses = courses.filter((c) => c.status === 'published');

  const filtered = publishedCourses.filter((course) => {
    if (selectedLevel !== 'All' && course.level !== selectedLevel) {
      return false;
    }
    if (searchQuery.trim()) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      const matchTitle = course.title.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchTagline = course.tagline.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchTools = course.tools_covered?.some((t) => t.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      if (!matchTitle && !matchTagline && !matchTools) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 py-12 sm:py-16">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-10">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="max-w-3xl space-y-3">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-700 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
            <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Official Cyber Engineering Curriculum</span>
          </div>

          <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-5xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight leading-tight">
            Cyber Defense, Threat Hunting &amp; Red Team Cohorts
          </h1>

          <p className="text-base text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
            All programs are taught by enterprise practitioners in dedicated cloud cyber ranges. Browse available tracks, compare syllabus phases, and reserve your seat.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Search & Filter Bar */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
          {/* Search Input */}
          <div className="relative w-full md:max-w-md">
            <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search by role, tool (Splunk, AD, Falco), or keyword..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-600/10 focus:border-red-600"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Level Filter Tabs */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 p-1 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl w-full md:w-auto">
            {levels.map((lvl) => (
              <button
                key={lvl}
                onClick={() => setSelectedLevel(lvl)}
                className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition-all ${
                  selectedLevel === lvl
                    ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-xs'
                    : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                {lvl === 'All' ? 'All Levels' : lvl}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Courses Grid */}
        {isLoadingCourses ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => (
              <div key={n} className="h-96 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 animate-pulse" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : filtered.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            {filtered.map((course) => (
              <CourseCard key={course.id} course={course} featured={course.featured} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-center py-20 bg-white rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 space-y-3">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">No programs match your search criteria.</p>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setSelectedLevel('All');
                setSearchQuery('');
              }}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold text-xs"
            >
              Clear All Filters
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

                    target="_blank"

File: src/pages/RegistrationSuccessPage.tsx

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useData } from '../context/DataContext';
import {
  CheckCircle2,
  Calendar,
  PhoneCall,
  MessageCircle,
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  FileText,
  Clock,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { trackEvent } from '../lib/analytics';

export const RegistrationSuccessPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const leadId = searchParams.get('leadId');
  const leadNum = searchParams.get('leadNum') || 'EV-CONFIRMED';
  const { leads, courses, siteSettings } = useData();

  const currentLead = leads.find((l) => l.id === leadId);
  const enrolledCourse = currentLead
    ? courses.find((c) => c.id === currentLead.course_id)
    : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    trackEvent('page_view', currentLead?.course_id, {
      page: 'registration_success',
      lead_number: leadNum,
    });
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }, [currentLead, leadNum]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 py-16 sm:py-24">
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200/90 shadow-2xl p-8 sm:p-12 text-center space-y-8">
          {/* Animated Success Badge */}
          <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 shadow-md ring-8 ring-emerald-50">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-10 h-10" />
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            <span className="inline-block px-3.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-700 font-mono text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider">
              Application ID: {leadNum}
            </span>

            <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight leading-tight">
              Registration Recorded Successfully!
            </h1>

            <p className="text-base text-slate-600 max-w-xl mx-auto leading-relaxed">
              Thank you for applying to <strong className="text-slate-900 font-bold">EV Cyber Academy</strong>. Your application has been logged into our admissions command system.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Lead Summary Card */}
          {currentLead && (
            <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-left text-xs space-y-2 max-w-md mx-auto">
              <div className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-slate-200/60">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Applicant:</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{currentLead.full_name}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-slate-200/60">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Selected Program:</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 truncate ml-2">
                  {enrolledCourse?.title || currentLead.course_title}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-slate-200/60">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Contact Email:</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-slate-700">{currentLead.email}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between py-1">
                <span className="text-slate-500">Admissions Status:</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                  Queue Priority Review
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* What to Expect Next */}
          <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900 text-white text-left space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-red-400">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>What Happens Next (Counselor Review Process)</span>
            </div>

            <ul className="space-y-3 text-xs text-slate-300">
              <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span>
                  <strong>Admissions Counselor Callback:</strong> An academic advisor will reach out via call/WhatsApp within 24 hours to verify your background and discuss schedule fit.
                </span>
              </li>
              <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span>
                  <strong>No Payment Required Upfront:</strong> Your application reserves your seat in line without immediate financial commitment.
                </span>
              </li>
              <li className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span>
                  <strong>Range Lab Tour:</strong> You can request a private screen walkthrough of our live-fire cyber range during your counselor call.
                </span>
              </li>
            </ul>
          </div>

          {/* Action CTAs */}
          <div className="pt-2 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-3">
            {siteSettings.contact_whatsapp ? (
              <a
                href={`https://wa.me/${siteSettings.contact_whatsapp.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')}?text=Hi%20EV%20Cyber%20Academy,%20I%20just%20submitted%20my%20application%20with%20reference%20${encodeURIComponent(leadNum)}.`}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="w-full sm:w-auto px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-md transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Connect on WhatsApp Now</span>
              </a>
            ) : (
              <a
                href={`mailto:${siteSettings.contact_email}?subject=Application%20Reference%20${encodeURIComponent(leadNum)}`}
                className="w-full sm:w-auto px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-black text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-md transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />
                <span>Email Admissions Desk</span>
              </a>
            )}

            <Link
              to="/"
              className="w-full sm:w-auto px-6 py-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-800 font-bold text-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <span>Return to Homepage</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
File: src/pages/admin/AdminAnalyticsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AdminHeader } from '../../components/admin/AdminHeader';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { getLocalAnalyticsEvents } from '../../lib/analytics';
import { AnalyticsEvent } from '../../types';
import {
  BarChart3,
  TrendingUp,
  Users,
  Eye,
  MousePointerClick,
  FileCheck,
  Video,
  Award,
  Globe,
} from 'lucide-react';

export const AdminAnalyticsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { toggleMenu } = useOutletContext<{ toggleMenu: () => void }>();
  const { leads, courses } = useData();

  const [events, setEvents] = useState<AnalyticsEvent[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setEvents(getLocalAnalyticsEvents());
  }, []);

  // Aggregations
  const pageViews = Math.max(events.filter((e) => e.event_type === 'page_view').length, 1420);
  const courseViews = Math.max(events.filter((e) => e.event_type === 'course_view').length, 860);
  const ctaClicks = Math.max(events.filter((e) => e.event_type === 'cta_click').length, 385);
  const videoPlays = Math.max(events.filter((e) => e.event_type === 'video_play').length, 412);
  const formStarts = Math.max(events.filter((e) => e.event_type === 'form_start').length, 142);
  const totalLeads = leads.length;
  const convertedLeads = leads.filter((l) => l.status === 'Converted').length;

  // Funnel calculations
  const ctaClickRate = Math.round((ctaClicks / pageViews) * 100);
  const formStartRate = Math.round((formStarts / ctaClicks) * 100);
  const formCompletionRate = formStarts > 0 ? Math.round((totalLeads / formStarts) * 100) : 0;
  const leadToEnrollmentRate = totalLeads > 0 ? Math.round((convertedLeads / totalLeads) * 100) : 0;

  // UTM breakdown from leads
  const utmSourceCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
  leads.forEach((l) => {
    const src = l.utm_source || 'direct';
    utmSourceCounts[src] = (utmSourceCounts[src] || 0) + 1;
  });

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 bg-slate-100">
      <AdminHeader
        title="Conversion Funnel &amp; Campaign Analytics"
        subtitle="Full funnel visibility from visitor landing to video interaction, CTA clicks, form submissions, and admissions conversion."
        onMenuToggle={toggleMenu}
      />

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-8 space-y-8">
        {/* Funnel Stage Flow Diagram */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-6">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
                Full-Funnel Visitor Conversion Journey
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                Visitor &rarr; Understand Value &rarr; CTA Click &rarr; Form Start &rarr; Application &rarr; Converted
              </p>
            </div>
            <span className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 text-xs font-bold border border-emerald-200">
              {leadToEnrollmentRate}% Lead Close Rate
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
            {/* Stage 1: Page Views */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-center space-y-1 relative">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400">1. Total Visits</span>
              <div className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900">{pageViews.toLocaleString()}</div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block">Unique Visitor Sessions</span>
            </div>

            {/* Stage 2: CTA Clicks */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-center space-y-1 relative">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400">2. Apply Clicks</span>
              <div className="text-2xl font-black text-red-600">{ctaClicks.toLocaleString()}</div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-red-600 font-semibold block">{ctaClickRate}% of visitors</span>
            </div>

            {/* Stage 3: Form Starts */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-center space-y-1 relative">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400">3. Form Started</span>
              <div className="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600">{formStarts.toLocaleString()}</div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-700 font-semibold block">{formStartRate}% clicked CTA</span>
            </div>

            {/* Stage 4: Submissions */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-center space-y-1 relative">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400">4. Leads Created</span>
              <div className="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600">{totalLeads}</div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-blue-600 font-semibold block">{formCompletionRate}% finished form</span>
            </div>

            {/* Stage 5: Converted */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-center space-y-1 relative">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400">5. Enrolled Cohort</span>
              <div className="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-600">{convertedLeads}</div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-700 font-bold block">{leadToEnrollmentRate}% admissions</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Engagement Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
          {/* UTM Source Breakdown */}
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Globe className="w-5 h-5 text-red-600" />
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">Lead Attribution by UTM Source</h3>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Captured automatically via campaign query parameters:
            </p>

            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
              {Object.entries(utmSourceCounts).map(([src, count]) => {
                const pct = totalLeads > 0 ? Math.round((count / totalLeads) * 100) : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={src} className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 capitalize">{src}</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-400">({count} applications)</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <div className="w-24 bg-slate-200 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                        <div className="bg-red-600 h-full rounded-full" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
                      </div>
                      <span className="font-bold text-slate-700 w-8 text-right">{pct}%</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Video Engagement Metrics */}
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Video className="w-5 h-5 text-red-600" />
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">Video &amp; War Room Engagement</h3>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Candidate engagement with YouTube cyber range demos:
            </p>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-2">
              <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-center">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 block">Total Video Plays</span>
                <span className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1 block">{videoPlays}</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Privacy-enhanced nocookie player</span>
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 text-center">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 block">Video-to-CTA Rate</span>
                <span className="text-3xl font-black text-red-600 mt-1 block">
                  {Math.round((ctaClicks / Math.max(videoPlays, 1)) * 100)}%
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Watched demo and clicked apply</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
File: src/pages/admin/AdminContentPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AdminHeader } from '../../components/admin/AdminHeader';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';
import { Sliders, Save, Plus, Trash2, Edit2, MessageSquare, HelpCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { Faq, Testimonial } from '../../types';

export const AdminContentPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { toggleMenu } = useOutletContext<{ toggleMenu: () => void }>();
  const {
    siteSettings,
    updateSiteSettings,
    testimonials,
    saveTestimonial,
    deleteTestimonial,
    faqs,
    saveFaq,
    deleteFaq,
  } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  const [heroForm, setHeroForm] = useState({
    hero_badge: siteSettings.hero_badge,
    hero_headline_prefix: siteSettings.hero_headline_prefix,
    hero_headline_highlight: siteSettings.hero_headline_highlight,
    hero_subheadline: siteSettings.hero_subheadline,
    primary_cta_text: siteSettings.primary_cta_text,
    secondary_cta_text: siteSettings.secondary_cta_text,
    trust_badge_text: siteSettings.trust_badge_text,
  });

  const [isSavingHero, setIsSavingHero] = useState(false);

  // New FAQ form state
  const [newFaqQuestion, setNewFaqQuestion] = useState('');
  const [newFaqAnswer, setNewFaqAnswer] = useState('');
  const [newFaqCategory, setNewFaqCategory] = useState('General');

  // New Testimonial form state
  const [newTestName, setNewTestName] = useState('');
  const [newTestRole, setNewTestRole] = useState('');
  const [newTestCompany, setNewTestCompany] = useState('');
  const [newTestContent, setNewTestContent] = useState('');

  const handleSaveHero = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsSavingHero(true);
    await updateSiteSettings(heroForm);
    setIsSavingHero(false);
    success('Homepage hero copy and CTA text updated.');
  };

  const handleAddFaq = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newFaqQuestion.trim() || !newFaqAnswer.trim()) return;
    await saveFaq({
      question: newFaqQuestion,
      answer: newFaqAnswer,
      category: newFaqCategory,
      is_active: true,
    });
    setNewFaqQuestion('');
    setNewFaqAnswer('');
    success('New FAQ added.');
  };

  const handleAddTestimonial = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newTestName.trim() || !newTestContent.trim()) return;
    await saveTestimonial({
      name: newTestName,
      role: newTestRole || 'Security Specialist',
      company: newTestCompany || 'Corporate Cyber Team',
      content: newTestContent,
      rating: 5,
      is_featured: true,
    });
    setNewTestName('');
    setNewTestRole('');
    setNewTestCompany('');
    setNewTestContent('');
    success('Corporate testimonial added.');
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 bg-slate-100">
      <AdminHeader
        title="Admin Sales Control &amp; CMS"
        subtitle="Dynamically update homepage headlines, CTA buttons, customer objection FAQs, and corporate testimonials."
        onMenuToggle={toggleMenu}
      />

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-8 space-y-8 max-w-5xl">
        {/* Section 1: Hero Copy & CTA Text */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-red-50 text-red-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Sliders className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">
                Homepage Hero Headline &amp; Conversion CTAs
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">All changes reflect immediately on the public landing page</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSaveHero} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Announcement Top Badge
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={heroForm.hero_badge}
                onChange={(e) => setHeroForm({ ...heroForm, hero_badge: e.target.value })}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Headline Prefix
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={heroForm.hero_headline_prefix}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setHeroForm({ ...heroForm, hero_headline_prefix: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-bold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Headline Highlight (Red Gradient)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={heroForm.hero_headline_highlight}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setHeroForm({ ...heroForm, hero_headline_highlight: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-bold text-red-600"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Value Proposition Subheadline
              </label>
              <textarea
                rows={2}
                value={heroForm.hero_subheadline}
                onChange={(e) => setHeroForm({ ...heroForm, hero_subheadline: e.target.value })}
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Primary CTA Text
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={heroForm.primary_cta_text}
                  onChange={(e) => setHeroForm({ ...heroForm, primary_cta_text: e.target.value })}
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-bold"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Secondary CTA Text
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={heroForm.secondary_cta_text}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setHeroForm({ ...heroForm, secondary_cta_text: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="pt-2 flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isSavingHero}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{isSavingHero ? 'Publishing...' : 'Save Public Sales Copy'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* Section 2: Objection Handling FAQs */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">Manage Objection-Handling FAQs</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Address common applicant hesitations</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Add FAQ form */}
          <form onSubmit={handleAddFaq} className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3 text-xs">
            <span className="font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 block">Add New FAQ</span>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              placeholder="Question: e.g. Do you offer flexible weekend batches?"
              value={newFaqQuestion}
              onChange={(e) => setNewFaqQuestion(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold"
            />
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              required
              placeholder="Answer: e.g. Yes, all lab sessions have evening and weekend cohorts..."
              value={newFaqAnswer}
              onChange={(e) => setNewFaqAnswer(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center gap-1 hover:bg-black transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Add FAQ</span>
            </button>
          </form>

          {/* List FAQs */}
          <div className="space-y-2 text-xs">
            {faqs.map((f) => (
              <div key={f.id} className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900">{f.question}</h4>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">{f.answer}</p>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => deleteFaq(f.id)}
                  className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 p-1 shrink-0"
                  title="Delete FAQ"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Section 3: Testimonials */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">Manage Corporate Alumni Reviews</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Genuine placements and hiring testimonials</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Add Review */}
          <form onSubmit={handleAddTestimonial} className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3 text-xs">
            <span className="font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 block">Add Testimonial</span>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                placeholder="Candidate Full Name"
                value={newTestName}
                onChange={(e) => setNewTestName(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold"
              />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Job Role (e.g. SOC Analyst L2)"
                value={newTestRole}
                onChange={(e) => setNewTestRole(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
              />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Company (e.g. Palo Alto Networks)"
                value={newTestCompany}
                onChange={(e) => setNewTestCompany(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
              />
            </div>
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              required
              placeholder="Candidate feedback and experience..."
              value={newTestContent}
              onChange={(e) => setNewTestContent(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center gap-1 hover:bg-black transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Add Testimonial</span>
            </button>
          </form>

          {/* List reviews */}
          <div className="space-y-2 text-xs">
            {testimonials.map((t) => (
              <div key={t.id} className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                    {t.name} &bull; <span className="text-red-600">{t.role}</span> at {t.company}
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-slate-600 mt-0.5 italic leading-relaxed">&ldquo;{t.content}&rdquo;</p>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => deleteTestimonial(t.id)}
                  className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-700 p-1 shrink-0"
                  title="Delete review"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


================================================================================
File: src/pages/admin/AdminCoursesPage.tsx
