import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AdminHeader } from '../../components/admin/AdminHeader';
import { CourseEditorModal } from '../../components/admin/CourseEditorModal';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';
import { Course } from '../../types';
import {
  BookOpen,
  Plus,
  Edit2,
  Trash2,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Clock,
  Laptop,
  ArrowUp,
  ArrowDown,
  Globe,
  Copy,
  Check,
  Zap,
} from 'lucide-react';

export const AdminCoursesPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { toggleMenu } = useOutletContext<{ toggleMenu: () => void }>();
  const { courses, saveCourse, deleteCourse, updateCourseOrder } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  const [isEditorOpen, setIsEditorOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingCourse, setEditingCourse] = useState<Course | null>(null);
  const [copiedCourseId, setCopiedCourseId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleCopyAdUrl = (course: Course) => {
    const origin = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : 'https://registration01.evcyberacademy.in';
    const url = `${origin}/${course.slug}`;
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      setCopiedCourseId(course.id);
      success(`Copied ad campaign URL: /${course.slug}`);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedCourseId(null), 2000);
    }
  };

  const handleCreate = () => {
    setEditingCourse(null);
    setIsEditorOpen(true);
  };

  const handleEdit = (course: Course) => {
    setEditingCourse(course);
    setIsEditorOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOrderChange = async (courseId: string, newPosition: number) => {
    const res = await updateCourseOrder(courseId, newPosition);
    if (res.success) {
      success(`Course moved to position #${newPosition}.`);
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to update course order', res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleStatus = async (course: Course) => {
    const nextStatus = course.status === 'published' ? 'draft' : 'published';
    const res = await saveCourse({ id: course.id, status: nextStatus });
    if (res.success) {
      success(`Course ${nextStatus === 'published' ? 'published' : 'hidden'}.`);
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to update status', res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (course: Course) => {
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to delete the course "${course.title}"?`)) {
      return;
    }
    const res = await deleteCourse(course.id);
    if (res.success) {
      success('Course deleted.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to delete course', res.error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 bg-slate-100">
      <AdminHeader
        title="Courses &amp; Programs"
        subtitle="Manage the courses displayed on the EV Cyber Academy Private Limited registration website."
        onMenuToggle={toggleMenu}
        actions={
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleCreate}
            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-md cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Add Course</span>
          </button>
        }
      />

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-8 space-y-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full">
        {courses.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-12 text-center space-y-3">
            <BookOpen className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 mx-auto" />
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-800">No courses created yet</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Click the Add Course button above to create your first training program.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                    <th className="py-3.5 px-4 w-44">Display Order</th>
                    <th className="py-3.5 px-4">Course Name</th>
                    <th className="py-3.5 px-4">Price (Now / Original)</th>
                    <th className="py-3.5 px-4">Duration</th>
                    <th className="py-3.5 px-4">Learning Mode</th>
                    <th className="py-3.5 px-4">Status</th>
                    <th className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 font-medium text-slate-700">
                  {courses.map((course) => {
                    const isPub = course.status === 'published';
                    const durationText = course.duration_text || (course.duration_weeks ? `${course.duration_weeks} Weeks` : 'Self-Paced');
                    const learningMode = course.learning_mode || course.tagline || 'Live Online';

                    return (
                      <tr key={course.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition">
                        {/* Display Order Controls */}
                        <td className="py-4 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <select
                              value={course.sort_order || 1}
                              onChange={(e) => handleOrderChange(course.id, Number(e.target.value))}
                              className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-black text-slate-900 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 cursor-pointer"
                              title="Select which card displays 1st, 2nd, 3rd, etc."
                            >
                              {courses.map((_, idx) => (
                                <option key={idx + 1} value={idx + 1}>
                                  Card #{idx + 1} {idx === 0 ? '(1st)' : idx === 1 ? '(2nd)' : idx === 2 ? '(3rd)' : ''}
                                </option>
                              ))}
                            </select>

                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={(course.sort_order || 1) <= 1}
                              onClick={() => handleOrderChange(course.id, (course.sort_order || 1) - 1)}
                              className="p-1 rounded-md text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-200 transition disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed cursor-pointer"
                              title="Move Up"
                            >
                              <ArrowUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            </button>

                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={(course.sort_order || 1) >= courses.length}
                              onClick={() => handleOrderChange(course.id, (course.sort_order || 1) + 1)}
                              className="p-1 rounded-md text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-200 transition disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed cursor-pointer"
                              title="Move Down"
                            >
                              <ArrowDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </td>

                        <td className="py-4 px-4 font-bold text-slate-900 max-w-sm">
                          <div>
                            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">{course.title}</span>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 line-clamp-1 mt-0.5 font-normal">
                              {course.summary}
                            </p>

                            {/* Active Meta Ad Campaign Route Slug & Copy Button */}
                            <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-900 text-red-400 font-mono text-[11px] font-bold border border-slate-800">
                                <Globe className="w-3 h-3 text-red-500" />
                                <span>/{course.slug}</span>
                              </span>

                              {course.auto_open_modal && (
                                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300 text-[10px] font-bold">
                                  <Zap className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-amber-600" />
                                  Auto-Form
                                </span>
                              )}

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => handleCopyAdUrl(course)}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 text-[11px] font-semibold transition cursor-pointer"
                                title={`Copy Meta Ad campaign link for /${course.slug}`}
                              >
                                {copiedCourseId === course.id ? (
                                  <>
                                    <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" />
                                    <span className="text-emerald-700 font-bold">Copied!</span>
                                  </>
                                ) : (
                                  <>
                                    <Copy className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-500" />
                                    <span>Copy Ad URL</span>
                                  </>
                                )}
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </td>

                        <td className="py-4 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                          <div className="font-mono">
                            <span className="font-black text-slate-900 text-xs">
                              ₹{(course.fee_amount || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                            </span>
                            {course.original_price && course.original_price > (course.fee_amount || 0) ? (
                              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 line-through">
                                ₹{course.original_price.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                              </div>
                            ) : null}
                          </div>
                        </td>

                        <td className="py-4 px-4 text-slate-600 whitespace-nowrap">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleEdit(course)}
                            className="flex items-center gap-2 group px-2 py-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 transition cursor-pointer text-left"
                            title="Click to edit program duration or details"
                          >
                            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-red-500 transition" />
                            <span className="font-bold text-slate-800 group-hover:text-red-600 transition">
                              {durationText}
                            </span>
                            <span
                              className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase ${
                                (course.duration_unit === 'Days' || durationText.toLowerCase().includes('day'))
                                  ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-300'
                                  : 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border border-blue-300'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {(course.duration_unit === 'Days' || durationText.toLowerCase().includes('day'))
                                ? 'Days'
                                : 'Weeks'}
                            </span>
                          </button>
                        </td>

                        <td className="py-4 px-4 text-slate-600">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <Laptop className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                            <span>{learningMode}</span>
                          </div>
                        </td>

                        <td className="py-4 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleToggleStatus(course)}
                            className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold border transition flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer ${
                              isPub
                                ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 hover:bg-emerald-100'
                                : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-200'
                            }`}
                            title="Click to toggle publish status"
                          >
                            {isPub ? (
                              <>
                                <Eye className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" />
                                <span>Published</span>
                              </>
                            ) : (
                              <>
                                <EyeOff className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-500" />
                                <span>Hidden</span>
                              </>
                            )}
                          </button>
                        </td>

                        <td className="py-4 px-4 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleEdit(course)}
                              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold transition flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                              <Edit2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                              <span>Edit</span>
                            </button>

                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleDelete(course)}
                              className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-600 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 transition cursor-pointer"
                              title="Delete Course"
                            >
                              <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Add / Edit Course Modal */}
      {isEditorOpen && (
        <CourseEditorModal
          course={editingCourse}
          onClose={() => setIsEditorOpen(false)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/pages/admin/AdminDashboardPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AdminHeader } from '../../components/admin/AdminHeader';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';
import { Lead, LeadStatus } from '../../types';
import { DeleteAllLeadsModal } from '../../components/admin/DeleteAllLeadsModal';
import {
  Users,
  UserCheck,
  BookOpen,
  Search,
  Download,
  Phone,
  MessageCircle,
  Mail,
  Trash2,
  Calendar,
  X,
  FileText,
  Plus,
  AlertCircle,
  Loader2,
  RefreshCw,
} from 'lucide-react';

export const AdminDashboardPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { toggleMenu } = useOutletContext<{ toggleMenu: () => void }>();
  const {
    leads,
    courses,
    isLoadingLeads,
    leadsFetchError,
    fetchLeads,
    updateLeadStatus,
    addLeadNote,
    getLeadNotes,
    deleteLead,
    deleteAllLeads,
    exportLeadsCsv,
  } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  // Fetch latest backend leads once on dashboard mount
  React.useEffect(() => {
    fetchLeads();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [selectedStatus, setSelectedStatus] = useState<string>('all');
  const [activeLeadForModal, setActiveLeadForModal] = useState<Lead | null>(null);
  const [newNoteText, setNewNoteText] = useState('');
  const [isSavingNote, setIsSavingNote] = useState(false);
  const [showDeleteAllModal, setShowDeleteAllModal] = useState(false);
  const [isDeletingAll, setIsDeletingAll] = useState(false);

  // 1. KPI Counts
  const totalApplications = leads.length;
  const newApplications = leads.filter((l) => l.status === 'New').length;
  const totalCourses = courses.length;

  // 2. Filter & Search
  const filteredLeads = leads.filter((lead) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      lead.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
      lead.email.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
      lead.phone.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
      (lead.course_title || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase());

    const matchesStatus =
      selectedStatus === 'all' ? true : lead.status === selectedStatus;

    return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
  });

  // Handle status update
  const handleStatusChange = async (leadId: string, newStatus: LeadStatus) => {
    const res = await updateLeadStatus(leadId, newStatus);
    if (res.success) {
      success(`Status updated to ${newStatus}.`);
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to update status', res.error);
    }
  };

  // Handle Delete
  const handleDeleteLead = async (lead: Lead) => {
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to delete the application for ${lead.full_name}?`)) {
      return;
    }
    const res = await deleteLead(lead.id);
    if (res.success) {
      if (activeLeadForModal?.id === lead.id) {
        setActiveLeadForModal(null);
      }
      success('Application deleted.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Delete failed', res.error);
    }
  };

  // Handle Delete All Leads
  const handleConfirmDeleteAll = async () => {
    if (leads.length === 0) return;
    setIsDeletingAll(true);
    const res = await deleteAllLeads();
    setIsDeletingAll(false);
    setShowDeleteAllModal(false);

    if (res.success) {
      if (activeLeadForModal) {
        setActiveLeadForModal(null);
      }
      success(`All leads deleted successfully (${res.count || 0} removed).`);
    } else {
      notifyError('Delete failed', res.error || 'Failed to delete leads.');
    }
  };

  // Handle Adding Internal Note
  const handleAddNote = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeLeadForModal || !newNoteText.trim()) return;

    setIsSavingNote(true);
    const res = await addLeadNote(activeLeadForModal.id, newNoteText.trim());
    setIsSavingNote(false);

    if (res.success) {
      setNewNoteText('');
      success('Internal note added.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to save note', res.error);
    }
  };

  // Helper for status badge colors
  const getStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'New':
        return 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-200';
      case 'Contacted':
        return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-200';
      case 'Interested':
        return 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border-purple-200';
      case 'Converted':
        return 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200';
      case 'Not Interested':
        return 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-200';
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-200';
    }
  };

  const activeNotes = activeLeadForModal ? getLeadNotes(activeLeadForModal.id) : [];

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 bg-slate-100">
      {/* Top Header */}
      <AdminHeader
        title="Course Applications"
        subtitle="Manage student registrations for EV Cyber Academy Private Limited."
        onMenuToggle={toggleMenu}
        actions={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={totalApplications === 0 || isDeletingAll}
              onClick={() => setShowDeleteAllModal(true)}
              className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-red-600 border border-red-200 text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
              title="Permanently delete all registered leads"
            >
              <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-600" />
              <span>Delete All Leads</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => exportLeadsCsv()}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-black text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Download className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400" />
              <span>Export CSV</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-8 space-y-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full">
        {/* =========================================================================
            1. 3 TOP KPI CARDS
        ========================================================================== */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 sm:gap-6">
          {/* Card 1: Total Applications */}
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                Total Applications
              </p>
              <h3 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1">
                {totalApplications}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">All registered students</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Users className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Card 2: New Applications */}
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                New Applications
              </p>
              <h3 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1">
                {newApplications}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-blue-600 font-semibold mt-0.5">
                Awaiting contact
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <UserCheck className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Card 3: Total Courses */}
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                Total Courses
              </p>
              <h3 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1">
                {totalCourses}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Programs in academy</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-purple-50 text-purple-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <BookOpen className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* =========================================================================
            2. APPLICATIONS SEARCH & TABLE
        ========================================================================== */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden space-y-4 p-4 sm:p-6">
          {/* Search & Filter Bar */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
            {/* Search Input */}
            <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-md">
              <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Search by student name, email, or number..."
                value={searchTerm}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Status Filter Tabs */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 sm:pb-0 text-xs">
              {['all', 'New', 'Contacted', 'Interested', 'Converted', 'Not Interested'].map((status) => (
                <button
                  key={status}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedStatus(status)}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-bold transition shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                    selectedStatus === status
                      ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white'
                      : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {status === 'all' ? 'All' : status}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Database Sync Error Banner */}
          {leadsFetchError && (
            <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 shrink-0" />
                <span>Backend Database Error: {leadsFetchError}</span>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fetchLeads()}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer shadow-xs"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Retry Database Sync</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Applications Table */}
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            {isLoadingLeads ? (
              <div className="py-16 text-center text-slate-500 space-y-3">
                <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 mx-auto animate-spin text-red-600" />
                <p className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">Retrieving Live Leads from Supabase Database...</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Connecting to persistent PostgreSQL backend</p>
              </div>
            ) : filteredLeads.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="py-16 text-center text-slate-400 space-y-2">
                <Users className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-slate-300" />
                <p className="font-bold text-slate-700 text-sm">No applications found</p>
                <p className="text-xs">
                  {searchTerm ? 'Try a different search keyword.' : 'Applications submitted by students will appear here.'}
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-y border-slate-200 text-slate-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Student Name</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Email</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Contact Number</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Selected Course</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Application Date</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Status</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 font-medium text-slate-700">
                  {filteredLeads.map((lead) => {
                    const matchedCourse = courses.find((c) => c.id === lead.course_id);
                    const courseName = matchedCourse?.title || lead.course_title || 'General Application';
                    const rawPhone = lead.phone.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');

                    return (
                      <tr key={lead.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition">
                        {/* 1. Student Name */}
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-bold text-slate-900">
                          <div>
                            <span>{lead.full_name}</span>
                            <span className="block text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono mt-0.5">
                              {lead.lead_number}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </td>

                        {/* 2. Email */}
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                          <a
                            href={`mailto:${lead.email}`}
                            className="text-slate-600 hover:text-red-600 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition"
                          >
                            <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                            <span>{lead.email}</span>
                          </a>
                        </td>

                        {/* 3. Contact Number */}
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <a
                              href={`tel:${lead.phone}`}
                              className="text-slate-800 font-semibold hover:text-red-600 flex items-center gap-1"
                              title="Call"
                            >
                              <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                              <span>{lead.phone}</span>
                            </a>

                            {rawPhone && (
                              <a
                                href={`https://wa.me/${rawPhone}?text=Hello%20${encodeURIComponent(lead.full_name)},%20this%20is%20EV%20Cyber%20Academy%20Private%20Limited%20regarding%20your%20course%20application.`}
                                target="_blank"
                                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                                className="p-1 rounded bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-100 transition"
                                title="Chat on WhatsApp"
                              >
                                <MessageCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                              </a>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </td>

                        {/* 4. Selected Course */}
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                          <span className="inline-block max-w-[200px] truncate font-semibold text-slate-900" title={courseName}>
                            {courseName}
                          </span>
                        </td>

                        {/* 5. Application Date */}
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {new Date(lead.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
                            month: 'short',
                            day: 'numeric',
                            year: 'numeric',
                          })}
                        </td>

                        {/* 6. Application Status Dropdown */}
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                          <select
                            value={lead.status}
                            onChange={(e) => handleStatusChange(lead.id, e.target.value as LeadStatus)}
                            className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold border focus:outline-none cursor-pointer ${getStatusBadge(
                              lead.status
                            )}`}
                          >
                            <option value="New">New</option>
                            <option value="Contacted">Contacted</option>
                            <option value="Interested">Interested</option>
                            <option value="Converted">Enrolled</option>
                            <option value="Not Interested">Closed</option>
                          </select>
                        </td>

                        {/* 7. Actions */}
                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setActiveLeadForModal(lead)}
                              className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1"
                              title="View & Add Notes"
                            >
                              <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                              <span>Notes</span>
                            </button>

                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleDeleteLead(lead)}
                              className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-600 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 transition cursor-pointer"
                              title="Delete Application"
                            >
                              <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =========================================================================
          3. APPLICATION DETAILS & INTERNAL NOTES MODAL
      ========================================================================== */}
      {activeLeadForModal && (
        <div
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6"
        >
          <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
            {/* Modal Header */}
            <div className="p-6 bg-slate-950 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-red-400 uppercase">
                  {activeLeadForModal.lead_number}
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">
                  {activeLeadForModal.full_name}
                </h3>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveLeadForModal(null)}
                className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 space-y-6 max-h-[75vh] overflow-y-auto text-xs">
              {/* Application Details Summary */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2">
                <div className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-slate-200/60">
                  <span className="text-slate-500 font-semibold">Course:</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                    {courses.find((c) => c.id === activeLeadForModal.course_id)?.title ||
                      activeLeadForModal.course_title}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-slate-200/60">
                  <span className="text-slate-500 font-semibold">Email:</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-slate-800">{activeLeadForModal.email}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-slate-200/60">
                  <span className="text-slate-500 font-semibold">Contact:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{activeLeadForModal.phone}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between py-1 border-b border-slate-200/60">
                  <span className="text-slate-500 font-semibold">Registered On:</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-700">
                    {new Date(activeLeadForModal.created_at).toLocaleString()}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center py-1">
                  <span className="text-slate-500 font-semibold">Status:</span>
                  <select
                    value={activeLeadForModal.status}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const newSt = e.target.value as LeadStatus;
                      handleStatusChange(activeLeadForModal.id, newSt);
                      setActiveLeadForModal({ ...activeLeadForModal, status: newSt });
                    }}
                    className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-bold border ${getStatusBadge(
                      activeLeadForModal.status
                    )}`}
                  >
                    <option value="New">New</option>
                    <option value="Contacted">Contacted</option>
                    <option value="Interested">Interested</option>
                    <option value="Converted">Enrolled</option>
                    <option value="Not Interested">Closed</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Internal Notes Section */}
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700">
                  <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600" />
                  <span>Internal Notes</span>
                </div>

                {/* Notes List */}
                <div className="space-y-2 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
                  {activeNotes.length === 0 ? (
                    <p className="text-slate-400 italic py-2">No internal notes added yet.</p>
                  ) : (
                    activeNotes.map((note) => (
                      <div
                        key={note.id}
                        className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1"
                      >
                        <p className="text-slate-800 text-xs leading-relaxed">{note.note}</p>
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 pt-1">
                          <span>{note.author_name}</span>
                          <span>{new Date(note.created_at).toLocaleString()}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Add Note Form */}
                <form onSubmit={handleAddNote} className="space-y-2 pt-2">
                  <textarea
                    rows={2}
                    placeholder="Add an internal follow-up note..."
                    value={newNoteText}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewNoteText(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 text-xs text-slate-800 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 resize-none"
                  />
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleDeleteLead(activeLeadForModal)}
                      className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>Delete Application</span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={isSavingNote || !newNoteText.trim()}
                      className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white font-bold text-xs transition flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>{isSavingNote ? 'Saving...' : 'Add Note'}</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Delete All Leads Confirmation Modal */}
      <DeleteAllLeadsModal
        isOpen={showDeleteAllModal}
        onClose={() => setShowDeleteAllModal(false)}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmDeleteAll}
        totalLeads={totalApplications}
        isDeleting={isDeletingAll}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
File: src/pages/admin/AdminLeadsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AdminHeader } from '../../components/admin/AdminHeader';
import { LeadTable } from '../../components/admin/LeadTable';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';
import { DeleteAllLeadsModal } from '../../components/admin/DeleteAllLeadsModal';
import { Download, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';

export const AdminLeadsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { toggleMenu } = useOutletContext<{ toggleMenu: () => void }>();
  const { leads, exportLeadsCsv, deleteAllLeads, fetchLeads } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  // Ensure fresh leads are fetched from database on mount
  React.useEffect(() => {
    fetchLeads();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  const [showDeleteModal, setShowDeleteModal] = useState(false);
  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

  const handleConfirmDeleteAll = async () => {
    if (leads.length === 0) return;
    setIsDeleting(true);
    const res = await deleteAllLeads();
    setIsDeleting(false);
    setShowDeleteModal(false);

    if (res.success) {
      success(`All leads deleted successfully (${res.count || 0} removed).`);
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to delete leads', res.error || 'Operation failed');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 bg-slate-100">
      <AdminHeader
        title="Lead CRM &amp; Pipeline Command"
        subtitle="Manage inquiries, counselor assignments, status transitions, and direct candidate outreach."
        onMenuToggle={toggleMenu}
        actions={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={leads.length === 0 || isDeleting}
              onClick={() => setShowDeleteModal(true)}
              className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-red-600 border border-red-200 text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
              title="Permanently delete all registered leads"
            >
              <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-600" />
              <span>Delete All Leads</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => exportLeadsCsv()}
              className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 hover:bg-black text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-400" />
              <span>Export CSV</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
        <LeadTable />
      </div>

      {/* Delete All Confirmation Modal */}
      <DeleteAllLeadsModal
        isOpen={showDeleteModal}
        onClose={() => setShowDeleteModal(false)}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmDeleteAll}
        totalLeads={leads.length}
        isDeleting={isDeleting}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

File: src/pages/admin/AdminSettingsPage.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AdminHeader } from '../../components/admin/AdminHeader';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';
import { isSupabaseConfigured } from '../../lib/supabase';
import {
  Settings,
  Video,
  Save,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  MapPin,
  Clock,
  Play,
  Sparkles,
  MousePointerClick,
} from 'lucide-react';

export const AdminSettingsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { toggleMenu } = useOutletContext<{ toggleMenu: () => void }>();
  const { siteSettings, updateSiteSettings, videos, saveVideo, courses } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  // Quick Apply Buttons Form (Admin Editable)
  const [buttonsForm, setButtonsForm] = useState({
    quick_btn1_text:
      siteSettings.quick_btn1_text || 'Apply for ₹499 - 14 Days Ethical Hacking Program',
    quick_btn1_course_id: siteSettings.quick_btn1_course_id || '',
    quick_btn1_course_name:
      siteSettings.quick_btn1_course_name || '14-Day Ethical Hacking Program',
    quick_btn1_enabled: siteSettings.quick_btn1_enabled !== false,
    quick_btn2_text:
      siteSettings.quick_btn2_text || 'Apply to Full Ethical Hacking Course',
    quick_btn2_course_id: siteSettings.quick_btn2_course_id || '',
    quick_btn2_course_name:
      siteSettings.quick_btn2_course_name || 'Full Ethical Hacking Course',
    quick_btn2_enabled: siteSettings.quick_btn2_enabled !== false,
    default_card_banner_text:
      siteSettings.default_card_banner_text || 'Professional Certification Track',
  });

  // Contact form
  const [contactForm, setContactForm] = useState({
    contact_phone: siteSettings.contact_phone || '',
    contact_email: siteSettings.contact_email || 'admissions@evcyberacademy.com',
    contact_whatsapp: siteSettings.contact_whatsapp || '',
    contact_address: siteSettings.contact_address || 'Bangalore & Online Worldwide',
    counseling_hours: siteSettings.counseling_hours || 'Mon - Sat: 9:00 AM - 8:00 PM IST',
  });

  // Promotional video form
  const currentHeroVideo = videos.find((v) => v.placement === 'hero' || v.pinned_homepage) || videos[0];
  const [videoUrl, setVideoUrl] = useState(
    currentHeroVideo?.youtube_url || 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=inWWhr5tnEA'
  );
  const [showIntroVideo, setShowIntroVideo] = useState(
    siteSettings.show_intro_video !== false
  );

  const [isSavingButtons, setIsSavingButtons] = useState(false);
  const [isSavingContact, setIsSavingContact] = useState(false);
  const [isSavingVideo, setIsSavingVideo] = useState(false);

  const handleSaveButtons = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsSavingButtons(true);
    const res = await updateSiteSettings(buttonsForm);
    setIsSavingButtons(false);
    if (res.success) {
      success('Quick apply buttons updated successfully.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to update quick apply buttons.');
    }
  };

  const handleSaveContact = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsSavingContact(true);
    const res = await updateSiteSettings(contactForm);
    setIsSavingContact(false);
    if (res.success) {
      success('Contact settings updated.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to update contact settings.');
    }
  };

  const handleSaveVideo = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsSavingVideo(true);

    // 1. Update siteSettings for intro video section visibility (On / Off)
    await updateSiteSettings({ show_intro_video: showIntroVideo });

    // 2. Save or update primary video if url is provided
    if (videoUrl.trim()) {
      const videoPayload = {
        ...(currentHeroVideo?.id ? { id: currentHeroVideo.id } : {}),
        title: 'EV Cyber Academy Overview',
        youtube_url: videoUrl.trim(),
        youtube_video_id: videoUrl.trim(),
        placement: 'hero' as const,
        pinned_homepage: true,
        is_active: showIntroVideo,
        autoplay: false,
        muted_autoplay: true,
        show_controls: true,
      };

      await saveVideo(videoPayload);
    }

    setIsSavingVideo(false);
    success(
      showIntroVideo
        ? 'Introduction video section enabled and updated.'
        : 'Introduction video section turned OFF (hidden from website).'
    );
  };

  const isConnected = isSupabaseConfigured();

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 bg-slate-100">
      <AdminHeader
        title="Settings"
        subtitle="Manage the action buttons, introduction video, contact details, and database status for EV Cyber Academy Private Limited."
        onMenuToggle={toggleMenu}
      />

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-8 space-y-8 max-w-4xl mx-auto w-full">
        {/* =========================================================================
            1. HOMEPAGE QUICK APPLY ACTION BUTTONS (ADMIN EDITABLE)
        ========================================================================== */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-red-100 text-red-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <MousePointerClick className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">
                Homepage Quick Apply Action Buttons
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                Configure the 2 primary call-to-action buttons displayed at the very top of the student website
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSaveButtons} className="space-y-6 text-xs">
            {/* Button 1 Configuration */}
            <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/80 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="font-black text-sm text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-red-600 inline-block" />
                  1st Button (Red Gradient Action)
                </span>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer font-bold text-slate-700">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={buttonsForm.quick_btn1_enabled}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setButtonsForm({ ...buttonsForm, quick_btn1_enabled: e.target.checked })
                    }
                    className="w-4 h-4 rounded text-red-600 focus:ring-red-500"
                  />
                  <span>Show Button 1</span>
                </label>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Button Label / Text
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={buttonsForm.quick_btn1_text}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setButtonsForm({ ...buttonsForm, quick_btn1_text: e.target.value })
                    }
                    placeholder="e.g. Apply for ₹499 - 14 Days Ethical Hacking Program"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 bg-white"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Linked Target Course
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={buttonsForm.quick_btn1_course_id}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const selected = courses.find((c) => c.id === e.target.value);
                      setButtonsForm({
                        ...buttonsForm,
                        quick_btn1_course_id: e.target.value,
                        quick_btn1_course_name: selected?.title || buttonsForm.quick_btn1_course_name,
                      });
                    }}
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 bg-white"
                  >
                    <option value="">-- Auto-Match 14-Day Program --</option>
                    {courses.map((c) => (
                      <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                        {c.title}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Button 2 Configuration */}
            <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/80 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="font-black text-sm text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-slate-900 inline-block" />
                  2nd Button (Corporate Slate Action)
                </span>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer font-bold text-slate-700">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={buttonsForm.quick_btn2_enabled}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setButtonsForm({ ...buttonsForm, quick_btn2_enabled: e.target.checked })
                    }
                    className="w-4 h-4 rounded text-red-600 focus:ring-red-500"
                  />
                  <span>Show Button 2</span>
                </label>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Button Label / Text
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={buttonsForm.quick_btn2_text}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setButtonsForm({ ...buttonsForm, quick_btn2_text: e.target.value })
                    }
                    placeholder="e.g. Apply to Full Ethical Hacking Course"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 bg-white"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Linked Target Course
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={buttonsForm.quick_btn2_course_id}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const selected = courses.find((c) => c.id === e.target.value);
                      setButtonsForm({
                        ...buttonsForm,
                        quick_btn2_course_id: e.target.value,
                        quick_btn2_course_name: selected?.title || buttonsForm.quick_btn2_course_name,
                      });
                    }}
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 bg-white"
                  >
                    <option value="">-- Auto-Match Full Course --</option>
                    {courses.map((c) => (
                      <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                        {c.title}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Global Course Card Hacker Banner Text */}
            <div className="p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/80 space-y-3">
              <span className="font-black text-sm text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-red-600 inline-block" />
                Course Card Hacker Banner Label (Default)
              </span>
              <div>
                <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Default Banner Badge Text
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={buttonsForm.default_card_banner_text}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setButtonsForm({ ...buttonsForm, default_card_banner_text: e.target.value })
                  }
                  placeholder="Professional Certification Track"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500 bg-white"
                />
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">
                  Displayed on the hacker-themed banner across all course cards (can also be customized per course in Course Management).
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isSavingButtons}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{isSavingButtons ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Settings'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* =========================================================================
            2. INTRODUCTION / PROMOTIONAL VIDEO
        ========================================================================== */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-red-50 text-red-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Video className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">
                Introduction / Promotional Video
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                Shown in the Introduction to EV Cyber Academy video section
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSaveVideo} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
            {/* Intro Video Section ON / OFF Toggle */}
            <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/90 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span
                    className={`w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full ${
                      showIntroVideo ? 'bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse' : 'bg-slate-400'
                    }`}
                  />
                  Intro Video Section: {showIntroVideo ? 'ON (Visible on Website)' : 'OFF (Hidden from Website)'}
                </span>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  {showIntroVideo
                    ? 'The promotional video section is currently shown on the single-page website.'
                    : 'The promotional video section is turned OFF and hidden from the website.'}
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowIntroVideo(!showIntroVideo)}
                className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer shrink-0 ${
                  showIntroVideo
                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white shadow-sm'
                    : 'bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-900 text-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                <span>{showIntroVideo ? 'Turn Video OFF' : 'Turn Video ON'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                YouTube Video URL or Video ID
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Play className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required={showIntroVideo}
                  placeholder="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=... or Video ID"
                  value={videoUrl}
                  onChange={(e) => setVideoUrl(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500"
                />
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Paste any standard YouTube watch link, share link, or video ID.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isSavingVideo}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{isSavingVideo ? 'Updating...' : 'Update Video Settings'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* =========================================================================
            3. OFFICIAL CONTACT INFORMATION
        ========================================================================== */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs space-y-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center">
              <Settings className="w-5 h-5 text-red-500" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-black text-slate-900">
                Contact Information
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                Displayed in the Contact Us section and footer of the website
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSaveContact} className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Contact Number
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Phone className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="e.g. +91 98765 43210"
                  value={contactForm.contact_phone}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setContactForm({ ...contactForm, contact_phone: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Official Email
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  placeholder="admissions@evcyberacademy.com"
                  value={contactForm.contact_email}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setContactForm({ ...contactForm, contact_email: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                WhatsApp Number (with Country Code)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="e.g. +919876543210"

                  setContactForm({ ...contactForm, contact_whatsapp: e.target.value })
                }
                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Office Hours
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Mon - Sat: 9:00 AM - 8:00 PM IST"
                  value={contactForm.counseling_hours}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setContactForm({ ...contactForm, counseling_hours: e.target.value })
                  }
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500/20 focus:border-red-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="sm:col-span-2">
              <label className="block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Office Location / Address
              </label>

                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="sm:col-span-2 pt-2 flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isSavingContact}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <Save className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{isSavingContact ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Contact Details'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* =========================================================================
            4. DATABASE CONNECTION STATUS
        ========================================================================== */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
          <div className="space-y-0.5">
            <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">Database Connection</p>
            <p className="text-slate-500">Supabase PostgreSQL Engine</p>
          </div>

          <span
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-bold border flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              isConnected
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200'
                : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200'
            }`}
          >
            {isConnected ? (
              <>
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Connected</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Local Storage Mode</span>
              </>
            )}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

File: src/pages/admin/AdminVideosPage.tsx
