import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AdminHeader } from '../../components/admin/AdminHeader';
import { VideoEditorModal } from '../../components/admin/VideoEditorModal';
import { useData } from '../../context/DataContext';
import { useNotification } from '../../context/NotificationContext';
import { VideoItem } from '../../types';
import { YouTubeEmbed } from '../../components/common/YouTubeEmbed';
import {
  Video,
  Plus,
  Edit2,
  Trash2,
  Play,
  VolumeX,
  Volume2,
  CheckCircle2,
  Pin,
  ExternalLink,
  Eye,
} from 'lucide-react';

export const AdminVideosPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { toggleMenu } = useOutletContext<{ toggleMenu: () => void }>();
  const { videos, courses, saveVideo, deleteVideo } = useData();
  const { success, error: notifyError } = useNotification();

  const [isEditorOpen, setIsEditorOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingVideo, setEditingVideo] = useState<VideoItem | null>(null);
  const [previewVideo, setPreviewVideo] = useState<VideoItem | null>(null);

  const handleCreate = () => {
    setEditingVideo(null);
    setIsEditorOpen(true);
  };

  const handleEdit = (video: VideoItem) => {
    setEditingVideo(video);
    setIsEditorOpen(true);
  };

  const handleToggleActive = async (video: VideoItem) => {
    const nextState = !video.is_active;
    const res = await saveVideo({ id: video.id, is_active: nextState });
    if (res.success) {
      success(`Video ${nextState ? 'enabled' : 'disabled'}.`);
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to update video status', res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleTogglePinHomepage = async (video: VideoItem) => {
    const nextState = !video.pinned_homepage;
    const res = await saveVideo({ id: video.id, pinned_homepage: nextState });
    if (res.success) {
      success(nextState ? 'Video pinned to homepage hero.' : 'Video unpinned from homepage.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Failed to update pin state', res.error);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (video: VideoItem) => {
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to remove video: "${video.title}"?`)) {
      return;
    }
    const res = await deleteVideo(video.id);
    if (res.success) {
      success('Video deleted.');
    } else {
      notifyError('Delete failed', res.error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 bg-slate-100">
      <AdminHeader
        title="Admin-Controlled YouTube Video Management"
        subtitle="Manage hero walkthroughs, course trailers, and privacy-enhanced video embeds with sound and autoplay controls."
        onMenuToggle={toggleMenu}
        actions={
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleCreate}
            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-md cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>Add YouTube Video</span>
          </button>
        }
      />

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
        {/* Guidelines alert */}
        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-start gap-3">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-red-50 text-red-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Video className="w-4 h-4" />
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 space-y-0.5">
            <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">Privacy-Enhanced &amp; Browser-Compliant Streaming</p>
            <p>
              All embeds automatically utilize the privacy-friendly <code className="font-mono text-red-600">youtube-nocookie.com</code> domain. Muted autoplay is enforced by default to respect modern Chromium &amp; Safari autoplay restrictions, and explicit unmute controls are provided to visitors.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Video Cards Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          {videos.map((video) => {
            const course = courses.find((c) => c.id === video.pinned_course_id);

            return (
              <div
                key={video.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-between"
              >
                {/* Video Header & Embed Component */}
                <div>
                  <div className="p-4 bg-slate-950 text-white flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                      {video.pinned_homepage && (
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider bg-red-600 text-white flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Pin className="w-3 h-3 fill-current" />
                          <span>Pinned to Homepage Hero</span>
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold uppercase bg-slate-800 text-slate-300">
                        Placement: {video.placement}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleToggleActive(video)}
                      className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold transition ${
                        video.is_active
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30'
                          : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {video.is_active ? 'Active' : 'Disabled'}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  {/* YouTube Embed Player */}
                  <div className="p-4">
                    <YouTubeEmbed
                      urlOrId={video.youtube_video_id || video.youtube_url}
                      title={video.title}
                      description={video.description}
                      autoplay={false}
                      muted={video.muted_autoplay}
                      showControls={video.show_controls}
                    />
                  </div>

                  {/* Meta Details */}
                  <div className="px-6 pb-4 space-y-2 text-xs">
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">{video.title}</h3>
                    {video.description && (
                      <p className="text-slate-500 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">{video.description}</p>
                    )}

                    <div className="pt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                      <span className="font-mono bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-slate-700">
                        ID: {video.youtube_video_id}
                      </span>
                      {course && (
                        <span className="bg-red-50 text-red-700 font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                          Linked to: {course.title}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <span className="text-slate-400">
                        Autoplay: {video.autoplay ? 'Yes (Muted)' : 'No'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Footer Actions */}
                <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleTogglePinHomepage(video)}
                      className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold border transition flex items-center gap-1 ${
                        video.pinned_homepage
                          ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200'
                          : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-100'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <Pin className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>{video.pinned_homepage ? 'Unpin Homepage' : 'Pin to Homepage'}</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleEdit(video)}
                      className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 hover:bg-black text-white text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Edit2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      <span>Edit Video</span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleDelete(video)}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-600 transition cursor-pointer"
                      title="Delete video"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Editor Modal */}
      {isEditorOpen && (
        <VideoEditorModal
          video={editingVideo}
          onClose={() => {
            setIsEditorOpen(false);
            setEditingVideo(null);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export type ExperienceLevel = 
  | 'Student / Fresher'
  | '0-2 Years IT/Dev'
  | '2-5 Years Tech/Security'
  | '5+ Years Professional'
  | 'Career Switcher';

export type LeadStatus = 
  | 'New'
  | 'Contacted'
  | 'Interested'
  | 'Converted'
  | 'Not Interested';

export type CourseLevel = 
  | 'Beginner'
  | 'Intermediate'
  | 'Advanced'
  | 'All Levels';

export type CourseStatus = 'published' | 'draft' | 'archived';

export type DurationUnit = 'Weeks' | 'Days';

export type VideoPlacement = 'hero' | 'after_hero' | 'course_details';

export interface SyllabusModule {

-- Enable UUID extension
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

-- 1. PROFILES (Staff & Admins)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.profiles (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('super_admin', 'staff')) DEFAULT 'staff',
    phone TEXT,
    avatar_url TEXT,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW()),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW())
);

-- 2. COURSES
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.courses (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    slug TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    tagline TEXT NOT NULL,
    summary TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    level TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (level IN ('Beginner', 'Intermediate', 'Advanced', 'All Levels')) DEFAULT 'Intermediate',
    duration_weeks INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 12,
    hours_per_week INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 8,
    fee_amount NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    currency TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INR',
    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('published', 'draft', 'archived')) DEFAULT 'published',
    featured BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    badge_text TEXT,
    certification_title TEXT NOT NULL,
    batch_start_date DATE,
    seats_remaining INT DEFAULT 20,
    syllabus JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    outcomes JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    prerequisites JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    tools_covered JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW()),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW())
);

-- 3. VIDEOS (Admin Controlled YouTube Management)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.videos (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    youtube_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    youtube_video_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    placement TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (placement IN ('hero', 'after_hero', 'course_details')) DEFAULT 'hero',
    pinned_homepage BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    pinned_course_id UUID REFERENCES public.courses(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    autoplay BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    muted_autoplay BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    show_controls BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    sort_order INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW()),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW())
);

-- 4. LEAD SOURCES
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.lead_sources (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW())
);

-- 5. LEADS
CREATE SEQUENCE IF NOT EXISTS lead_number_seq START WITH 1001;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.leads (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    lead_number TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL DEFAULT ('EV-' || NEXTVAL('lead_number_seq')::TEXT),
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    email TEXT NOT NULL,
    phone TEXT NOT NULL,
    course_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.courses(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    college_company TEXT NOT NULL,
    experience_level TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (experience_level IN ('Student / Fresher', '0-2 Years IT/Dev', '2-5 Years Tech/Security', '5+ Years Professional', 'Career Switcher')),
    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('New', 'Contacted', 'Interested', 'Converted', 'Not Interested')) DEFAULT 'New',
    assigned_to UUID REFERENCES public.profiles(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    next_follow_up_date DATE,
    referral_source TEXT,
    utm_source TEXT,
    utm_medium TEXT,
    utm_campaign TEXT,
    utm_term TEXT,
    utm_content TEXT,
    last_contacted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW()),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW())
);

-- Index for lead lookup & anti-duplicate check
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_email ON public.leads(email);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_course_id ON public.leads(course_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_status ON public.leads(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_created_at ON public.leads(created_at DESC);

-- 6. LEAD NOTES
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.lead_notes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),

);

);

    is_featured BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW())
);

-- 10. FAQS (Admin Managed)

    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    category TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'General',
    question TEXT NOT NULL,
    answer TEXT NOT NULL,
    course_id UUID REFERENCES public.courses(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    sort_order INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

);

    page_path TEXT NOT NULL,

-- ==============================================================================
-- AUTOMATIC PROFILE CREATION TRIGGER
-- ==============================================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, email, full_name, role)
    VALUES (
        new.id,
        new.email,
        COALESCE(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', split_part(new.email, '@', 1)),
        COALESCE(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'role', 'staff')
    )
    ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE
    SET email = EXCLUDED.email,
        full_name = EXCLUDED.full_name;
    RETURN new;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS on_auth_user_created ON auth.users;
CREATE TRIGGER on_auth_user_created
    AFTER INSERT ON auth.users
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user();

-- Helper functions for role checking
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_super_admin()
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM public.profiles
        WHERE id = auth.uid() AND role = 'super_admin' AND is_active = TRUE
    );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_staff()
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM public.profiles
        WHERE id = auth.uid() AND is_active = TRUE
    );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Profiles: Authenticated users can read their own or staff directory; Super admins manage all
CREATE POLICY "Public staff directory read" ON public.profiles
    FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (TRUE);

CREATE POLICY "Super admin manage profiles" ON public.profiles
    FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (public.is_super_admin());

-- Courses: Anyone can read published courses; Staff/Admin can manage
CREATE POLICY "Anyone can view published courses" ON public.courses
    FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated
    USING (status = 'published' OR public.is_staff());

CREATE POLICY "Staff manage courses" ON public.courses
    FOR ALL TO authenticated
    USING (public.is_staff());

-- Videos: Anyone can view active videos; Staff/Admin can manage
CREATE POLICY "Anyone can view active videos" ON public.videos
    FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated
    USING (is_active = TRUE OR public.is_staff());

CREATE POLICY "Staff manage videos" ON public.videos
    FOR ALL TO authenticated
    USING (public.is_staff());

-- Testimonials & FAQs: Anyone can view active
CREATE POLICY "Anyone can view testimonials" ON public.testimonials
    FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated USING (TRUE);

CREATE POLICY "Staff manage testimonials" ON public.testimonials
    FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (public.is_staff());

CREATE POLICY "Anyone can view active faqs" ON public.faqs
    FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated USING (is_active = TRUE OR public.is_staff());

CREATE POLICY "Staff manage faqs" ON public.faqs
    FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (public.is_staff());

-- Site settings: Anyone can read; Staff can manage
CREATE POLICY "Anyone can read site settings" ON public.site_settings
    FOR SELECT TO anon, authenticated USING (TRUE);

CREATE POLICY "Staff manage site settings" ON public.site_settings
    FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (public.is_staff());

-- Leads: Public can INSERT only (registration form); Staff can SELECT / UPDATE
CREATE POLICY "Public can submit leads" ON public.leads
    FOR INSERT TO anon, authenticated
    WITH CHECK (TRUE);

CREATE POLICY "Staff view leads" ON public.leads
    FOR SELECT TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.is_super_admin() 
        OR assigned_to = auth.uid()
        OR assigned_to IS NULL
    );

CREATE POLICY "Staff update leads" ON public.leads
    FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
    USING (
        public.is_super_admin() 
        OR assigned_to = auth.uid()
        OR assigned_to IS NULL
    );

CREATE POLICY "Super admin delete leads" ON public.leads
    FOR DELETE TO authenticated
    USING (public.is_super_admin());

-- Lead Notes: Staff can view and add notes
CREATE POLICY "Staff view notes" ON public.lead_notes
    FOR SELECT TO authenticated
    USING (public.is_staff());

CREATE POLICY "Staff insert notes" ON public.lead_notes
    FOR INSERT TO authenticated
    WITH CHECK (public.is_staff());

-- Lead Status History & Assignments: Staff can view and insert
CREATE POLICY "Staff view history" ON public.lead_status_history
    FOR SELECT TO authenticated
    USING (public.is_staff());

CREATE POLICY "Staff insert history" ON public.lead_status_history
    FOR INSERT TO authenticated
    WITH CHECK (public.is_staff());

CREATE POLICY "Staff view assignments" ON public.lead_assignments
    FOR SELECT TO authenticated
    USING (public.is_staff());

CREATE POLICY "Staff insert assignments" ON public.lead_assignments
    FOR INSERT TO authenticated
    WITH CHECK (public.is_staff());

-- Analytics: Public can insert events; Staff can view
CREATE POLICY "Public insert analytics" ON public.analytics_events
    FOR INSERT TO anon, authenticated
    WITH CHECK (TRUE);

CREATE POLICY "Staff view analytics" ON public.analytics_events
    FOR SELECT TO authenticated
    USING (public.is_staff());



-- ==============================================================================
-- SECURE RPC FOR ANTI-DUPLICATE CHECK WITHOUT EXPOSING STUDENT DATA
-- ==============================================================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.check_duplicate_registration(p_email TEXT, p_course_id UUID)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM public.leads
        WHERE lower(trim(email)) = lower(trim(p_email))
          AND course_id = p_course_id
          AND created_at >= (NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days')
    );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.check_duplicate_registration(TEXT, UUID) TO anon, authenticated;
